{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SE9W4hAXyvEwtjJYtQx5uX","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# ADR-003: Offline Synchronization Strategy\n     3→\n     4→*Status: Accepted*  \n     5→*Date: 2024-04-20*  \n     6→*Authors: Core Architecture Team*\n     7→\n     8→---\n     9→\n    10→## 1. Context\n    11→\n    12→TycoonVerse must provide a first-class **offline experience** while preserving\n    13→authoritative, server-side game state that supports competitive leaderboards,\n    14→IAP validation, cloud saves, and live-ops events.  \n    15→Players frequently enter connectivity dead-zones (e.g., subways, planes), yet\n    16→still expect deterministic economic simulations and the ability to queue\n    17→actions (e.g., factory upgrades, IPO filings).\n    18→\n    19→Key constraints:\n    20→\n    21→* Mobile platforms (iOS/Android) with unpredictable network quality.\n    22→* **SQLite** on-device storage is our single source of truth while offline.\n    23→* **Unity** runs on the main thread; long-running I/O must be asynchronous.\n    24→* Conflicts must be resolved deterministically to prevent economic exploits.\n    25→* Payload size and battery consumption must remain minimal.\n    26→* GDPR/CCPA mandates that personally identifiable information (PII) never\n    27→  leaves the device unless encrypted and explicitly consented.\n    28→\n    29→Previous spikes demonstrated that naïve “last-write-wins” led to inventory\n    30→duplication and negative cash balances when two devices were used concurrently.\n    31→\n    32→## 2. Decision\n    33→\n    34→We will implement a **Client-Side Event Sourcing** model paired with a\n    35→**CRDT-inspired merge** algorithm. The strategy combines:\n    36→\n    37→1. **Event Store (Local)**  \n    38→   * Append-only SQLite table `GameEventLog` captures every domain event with\n    39→     metadata (`PlayerId`, `Sequence`, `VectorClock`, `Checksum`).\n    40→2. **Snapshot Cache (Local)**  \n    41→   * Periodic materialized views of the domain state reduce boot-time replay.\n    42→3. **Sync Sessions (Bidirectional)**  \n    43→   * `SyncSessionId` groups a batch of events exchanged with the server,\n    44→     allowing resume/retry on flaky networks.\n    45→4. **Conflict-free Resolution**  \n    46→   * Commutative/associative reducers (inspired by Λ-CRDTs) guarantee identical\n    47→     results regardless of merge order.\n    48→5. **Idempotent REST Endpoints**  \n    49→   * Server exposes `/events/ingest` and `/events/pull` with cursor semantics.\n    50→6. **Deterministic Roll-Forward**  \n    51→   * Server is always **source of authority**; client replays any corrected\n    52→     events after reconciliation.\n    53→\n    54→### Architectural Patterns Utilized\n    55→\n    56→* **Repository Pattern** — isolates SQLite implementation from domain logic.\n    57→* **Observer Pattern** — domain aggregates publish events consumed by UI.\n    58→* **Factory Pattern** — instantiates platform-specific crypto & storage drivers.\n    59→* **Singleton** — `OfflineSyncManager` coordinates sessions game-wide.\n    60→* **MVVM** — view-models react to merged state for instant UI refresh.\n    61→\n    62→## 3. Rationale\n    63→\n    64→* **Event sourcing** retains full audit history and supports time-travel debug.\n    65→* CRDT-style merges avoid locking or complex three-way diffs.\n    66→* Vector clocks are lightweight (single 64-bit unsigned integer per device) yet\n    67→  sufficient because TycoonVerse enforces *sequential* local event creation.\n    68→* Idempotent ingestion eliminates duplicate processing on retry.\n    69→\n    70→## 4. Detailed Design\n    71→\n    72→### 4.1 Sequence Flow\n    73→\n    74→```mermaid\n    75→sequenceDiagram\n    76→    participant Client\n    77→    participant Server\n    78→\n    79→    Client->>Client: Add Event (Sequence N)\n    80→    Client->>SQLite: Append GameEventLog\n    81→    Client->>Client: Attempt Sync\n    82→    alt Online\n    83→        Client->>Server: POST /events/ingest\n    84→        Server-->>Client: 202 Accepted (SyncSessionId)\n    85→        Server->>Server: Persist & Validate\n    86→        loop Until Catch-Up\n    87→            Client->>Server: GET /events/pull?since=cursor\n    88→            Server-->>Client: Events Δ\n    89→            Client->>Client: Merge & Persist\n    90→        end\n    91→    else Offline\n    92→        Client->>Client: Queue SyncSession for retry\n    93→    end\n    94→    Client-->>UI: Publish New State\n    95→```\n    96→\n    97→### 4.2 Data Model (SQLite)\n    98→\n    99→```sql\n   100→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS GameEventLog (\n   101→    Id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n   102→    PlayerId        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n   103→    DeviceId        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n   104→    Sequence        INTEGER NOT NULL,\n   105→    VectorClock     INTEGER NOT NULL,\n   106→    EventType       TEXT    NOT NULL,\n   107→    PayloadJson     TEXT    NOT NULL,\n   108→    Checksum        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n   109→    CreatedUtc      TEXT    NOT NULL,\n   110→    SyncSessionId   TEXT    NULL,\n   111→    UNIQUE(PlayerId, DeviceId, Sequence)\n   112→);\n   113→\n   114→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS IX_GameEventLog_SyncSessionId\n   115→    ON GameEventLog (SyncSessionId);\n   116→```\n   117→\n   118→### 4.3 Core C# Components\n   119→\n   120→```csharp\n   121→namespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Sync\n   122→{\n   123→    using System;\n   124→    using System.Collections.Generic;\n   125→    using System.Net.Http;\n   126→    using System.Threading;\n   127→    using System.Threading.Tasks;\n   128→    using TycoonVerse.Domain.Events;\n   129→    using TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Persistence;\n   130→    using TycoonVerse.Shared;\n   131→\n   132→    /// <summary>\n   133→    /// Coordinates offline event capture, persistence, and eventual sync.\n   134→    /// Singleton is resolved via DI container (Lifetime.Singleton).\n   135→    /// </summary>\n   136→    public sealed class OfflineSyncManager : IOfflineSyncManager, IDisposable\n   137→    {\n   138→        private readonly IEventRepository _eventRepository;\n   139→        private readonly IHttpClientFactory _httpFactory;\n   140→        private readonly IClock _clock;\n   141→        private readonly ILogger _logger;\n   142→        private readonly CancellationTokenSource _cts = new();\n   143→\n   144→        private Task? _backgroundTask;\n   145→\n   146→        public OfflineSyncManager(\n   147→            IEventRepository eventRepository,\n   148→            IHttpClientFactory httpFactory,\n   149→            IClock clock,\n   150→            ILogger logger)\n   151→        {\n   152→            _eventRepository = eventRepository;\n   153→            _httpFactory     = httpFactory;\n   154→            _clock           = clock;\n   155→            _logger          = logger;\n   156→        }\n   157→\n   158→        public void Start()\n   159→        {\n   160→            _backgroundTask ??= Task.Run(PumpAsync, _cts.Token);\n   161→            _logger.Information(\"OfflineSyncManager started.\");\n   162→        }\n   163→\n   164→        public async Task EnqueueAsync(DomainEvent @event, CancellationToken ct = default)\n   165→        {\n   166→            var record = EventRecord.FromDomainEvent(@event, _clock.UtcNow);\n   167→            await _eventRepository.InsertAsync(record, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   168→        }\n   169→\n   170→        private async Task PumpAsync()\n   171→        {\n   172→            var token = _cts.Token;\n   173→\n   174→            while (!token.IsCancellationRequested)\n   175→            {\n   176→                try\n   177→                {\n   178→                    if (NetworkState.IsOnline)\n   179→                    {\n   180→                        await PushAsync(token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   181→                        await PullAsync(token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   182→                    }\n   183→                }\n   184→                catch (Exception ex)\n   185→                {\n   186→                    _logger.Error(ex, \"Sync cycle failed.\");\n   187→                }\n   188→\n   189→                await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15), token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   190→            }\n   191→        }\n   192→\n   193→        private async Task PushAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n   194→        {\n   195→            IReadOnlyCollection<EventRecord> pending =\n   196→                await _eventRepository.FetchUnsentAsync(512, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   197→\n   198→            if (pending.Count == 0) return;\n   199→\n   200→            using var client = _httpFactory.CreateClient(\"GameApi\");\n   201→            var sessionId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString(\"N\");\n   202→\n   203→            var payload = new SyncPayload(pending, sessionId);\n   204→\n   205→            HttpResponseMessage response =\n   206→                await client.PostAsJsonAsync(\"/events/ingest\", payload, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   207→\n   208→            if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode)\n   209→            {\n   210→                await _eventRepository.MarkAsSentAsync(pending, sessionId, ct)\n   211→                                       .ConfigureAwait(false);\n   212→\n   213→                _logger.Debug(\"Pushed {Count} events in session {Session}.\",\n   214→                              pending.Count, sessionId);\n   215→            }\n   216→            else\n   217→            {\n   218→                _logger.Warning(\"Push failed (HTTP {Status}). Retrying later.\",\n   219→                                 response.StatusCode);\n   220→            }\n   221→        }\n   222→\n   223→        private async Task PullAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n   224→        {\n   225→            using var client = _httpFactory.CreateClient(\"GameApi\");\n   226→\n   227→            long cursor = await _eventRepository.GetLastServerSequenceAsync(ct)\n   228→                                                .ConfigureAwait(false);\n   229→\n   230→            HttpResponseMessage response =\n   231→                await client.GetAsync($\"/events/pull?since={cursor}\", ct)\n   232→                            .ConfigureAwait(false);\n   233→\n   234→            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)\n   235→            {\n   236→                _logger.Warning(\"Pull failed (HTTP {Status}).\", response.StatusCode);\n   237→                return;\n   238→            }\n   239→\n   240→            var delta = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<EventDelta>(ct)\n   241→                                              .ConfigureAwait(false);\n   242→\n   243→            if (delta?.Events is {Count: > 0})\n   244→            {\n   245→                await _eventRepository.UpsertServerEventsAsync(delta.Events, ct)\n   246→                                       .ConfigureAwait(false);\n   247→\n   248→                DomainEvents.Raise(new ServerSyncApplied(delta.Events));\n   249→\n   250→                _logger.Debug(\"Pulled {Count} new events from server.\", delta.Events.Count);\n   251→            }\n   252→        }\n   253→\n   254→        public void Dispose()\n   255→        {\n   256→            _cts.Cancel();\n   257→            _backgroundTask?.Wait(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));\n   258→            _cts.Dispose();\n   259→        }\n   260→    }\n   261→}\n   262→```\n   263→\n   264→### 4.4 Error Handling & Telemetry\n   265→\n   266→* All sync failures are logged via the **crash-reporting** layer.\n   267→* Repeated (5×) failures trip a circuit breaker; UI shows “Unable to Sync.”\n   268→* A/B tests control back-off intervals via the analytics layer.\n   269→\n   270→## 5. Consequences\n   271→\n   272→Positive:\n   273→\n   274→* Seamless offline play; users rarely notice connectivity transitions.\n   275→* Deterministic state ensures fair competitive gameplay.\n   276→* Full audit trail simplifies cheat detection and rollback capabilities.\n   277→\n   278→Negative:\n   279→\n   280→* Event log may bloat; mitigation requires periodic compression.\n   281→* Increased implementation complexity versus naïve approaches.\n   282→* Requires rigorously defined event schema versioning.\n   283→\n   284→## 6. References\n   293→\n   294→* “Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types.” Shapiro et al. _SSS 2011._\n   295→* _Domain-Driven Design_ — Eric Evans.\n   296→* Internal Tech Spec `SYNC-01` for vector clock format.\n   297→\n   298→---\n   299→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"52b95c45-8169-4e21-826d-99ed2f9dc283","uuid":"f0ccb949-ab19-4eb8-903c-9693ce7c3c1e","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/project/docs/design/ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md","content":"```markdown\n# ADR-003: Offline Synchronization Strategy\n\n*Status: Accepted*  \n*Date: 2024-04-20*  \n*Authors: Core Architecture Team*\n\n---\n\n## 1. Context\n\nTycoonVerse must provide a first-class **offline experience** while preserving\nauthoritative, server-side game state that supports competitive leaderboards,\nIAP validation, cloud saves, and live-ops events.  \nPlayers frequently enter connectivity dead-zones (e.g., subways, planes), yet\nstill expect deterministic economic simulations and the ability to queue\nactions (e.g., factory upgrades, IPO filings).\n\nKey constraints:\n\n* Mobile platforms (iOS/Android) with unpredictable network quality.\n* **SQLite** on-device storage is our single source of truth while offline.\n* **Unity** runs on the main thread; long-running I/O must be asynchronous.\n* Conflicts must be resolved deterministically to prevent economic exploits.\n* Payload size and battery consumption must remain minimal.\n* GDPR/CCPA mandates that personally identifiable information (PII) never\n  leaves the device unless encrypted and explicitly consented.\n\nPrevious spikes demonstrated that naïve “last-write-wins” led to inventory\nduplication and negative cash balances when two devices were used concurrently.\n\n## 2. Decision\n\nWe will implement a **Client-Side Event Sourcing** model paired with a\n**CRDT-inspired merge** algorithm. The strategy combines:\n\n1. **Event Store (Local)**  \n   * Append-only SQLite table `GameEventLog` captures every domain event with\n     metadata (`PlayerId`, `Sequence`, `VectorClock`, `Checksum`).\n2. **Snapshot Cache (Local)**  \n   * Periodic materialized views of the domain state reduce boot-time replay.\n3. **Sync Sessions (Bidirectional)**  \n   * `SyncSessionId` groups a batch of events exchanged with the server,\n     allowing resume/retry on flaky networks.\n4. **Conflict-free Resolution**  \n   * Commutative/associative reducers (inspired by Λ-CRDTs) guarantee identical\n     results regardless of merge order.\n5. **Idempotent REST Endpoints**  \n   * Server exposes `/events/ingest` and `/events/pull` with cursor semantics.\n6. **Deterministic Roll-Forward**  \n   * Server is always **source of authority**; client replays any corrected\n     events after reconciliation.\n\n### Architectural Patterns Utilized\n\n* **Repository Pattern** — isolates SQLite implementation from domain logic.\n* **Observer Pattern** — domain aggregates publish events consumed by UI.\n* **Factory Pattern** — instantiates platform-specific crypto & storage drivers.\n* **Singleton** — `OfflineSyncManager` coordinates sessions game-wide.\n* **MVVM** — view-models react to merged state for instant UI refresh.\n\n## 3. Rationale\n\n* **Event sourcing** retains full audit history and supports time-travel debug.\n* CRDT-style merges avoid locking or complex three-way diffs.\n* Vector clocks are lightweight (single 64-bit unsigned integer per device) yet\n  sufficient because TycoonVerse enforces *sequential* local event creation.\n* Idempotent ingestion eliminates duplicate processing on retry.\n\n## 4. Detailed Design\n\n### 4.1 Sequence Flow\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Client\n    participant Server\n\n    Client->>Client: Add Event (Sequence N)\n    Client->>SQLite: Append GameEventLog\n    Client->>Client: Attempt Sync\n    alt Online\n        Client->>Server: POST /events/ingest\n        Server-->>Client: 202 Accepted (SyncSessionId)\n        Server->>Server: Persist & Validate\n        loop Until Catch-Up\n            Client->>Server: GET /events/pull?since=cursor\n            Server-->>Client: Events Δ\n            Client->>Client: Merge & Persist\n        end\n    else Offline\n        Client->>Client: Queue SyncSession for retry\n    end\n    Client-->>UI: Publish New State\n```\n\n### 4.2 Data Model (SQLite)\n\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS GameEventLog (\n    Id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    PlayerId        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    DeviceId        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    Sequence        INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    VectorClock     INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    EventType       TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    PayloadJson     TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    Checksum        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    CreatedUtc      TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    SyncSessionId   TEXT    NULL,\n    UNIQUE(PlayerId, DeviceId, Sequence)\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS IX_GameEventLog_SyncSessionId\n    ON GameEventLog (SyncSessionId);\n```\n\n### 4.3 Core C# Components\n\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Sync\n{\n    using System;\n    using System.Collections.Generic;\n    using System.Net.Http;\n    using System.Threading;\n    using System.Threading.Tasks;\n    using TycoonVerse.Domain.Events;\n    using TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Persistence;\n    using TycoonVerse.Shared;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Coordinates offline event capture, persistence, and eventual sync.\n    /// Singleton is resolved via DI container (Lifetime.Singleton).\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class OfflineSyncManager : IOfflineSyncManager, IDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly IEventRepository _eventRepository;\n        private readonly IHttpClientFactory _httpFactory;\n        private readonly IClock _clock;\n        private readonly ILogger _logger;\n        private readonly CancellationTokenSource _cts = new();\n\n        private Task? _backgroundTask;\n\n        public OfflineSyncManager(\n            IEventRepository eventRepository,\n            IHttpClientFactory httpFactory,\n            IClock clock,\n            ILogger logger)\n        {\n            _eventRepository = eventRepository;\n            _httpFactory     = httpFactory;\n            _clock           = clock;\n            _logger          = logger;\n        }\n\n        public void Start()\n        {\n            _backgroundTask ??= Task.Run(PumpAsync, _cts.Token);\n            _logger.Information(\"OfflineSyncManager started.\");\n        }\n\n        public async Task EnqueueAsync(DomainEvent @event, CancellationToken ct = default)\n        {\n            var record = EventRecord.FromDomainEvent(@event, _clock.UtcNow);\n            await _eventRepository.InsertAsync(record, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        private async Task PumpAsync()\n        {\n            var token = _cts.Token;\n\n            while (!token.IsCancellationRequested)\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    if (NetworkState.IsOnline)\n                    {\n                        await PushAsync(token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                        await PullAsync(token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    }\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.Error(ex, \"Sync cycle failed.\");\n                }\n\n                await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15), token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private async Task PushAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            IReadOnlyCollection<EventRecord> pending =\n                await _eventRepository.FetchUnsentAsync(512, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            if (pending.Count == 0) return;\n\n            using var client = _httpFactory.CreateClient(\"GameApi\");\n            var sessionId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString(\"N\");\n\n            var payload = new SyncPayload(pending, sessionId);\n\n            HttpResponseMessage response =\n                await client.PostAsJsonAsync(\"/events/ingest\", payload, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode)\n            {\n                await _eventRepository.MarkAsSentAsync(pending, sessionId, ct)\n                                       .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                _logger.Debug(\"Pushed {Count} events in session {Session}.\",\n                              pending.Count, sessionId);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                _logger.Warning(\"Push failed (HTTP {Status}). Retrying later.\",\n                                 response.StatusCode);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private async Task PullAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            using var client = _httpFactory.CreateClient(\"GameApi\");\n\n            long cursor = await _eventRepository.GetLastServerSequenceAsync(ct)\n                                                .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            HttpResponseMessage response =\n                await client.GetAsync($\"/events/pull?since={cursor}\", ct)\n                            .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)\n            {\n                _logger.Warning(\"Pull failed (HTTP {Status}).\", response.StatusCode);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            var delta = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<EventDelta>(ct)\n                                              .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            if (delta?.Events is {Count: > 0})\n            {\n                await _eventRepository.UpsertServerEventsAsync(delta.Events, ct)\n                                       .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                DomainEvents.Raise(new ServerSyncApplied(delta.Events));\n\n                _logger.Debug(\"Pulled {Count} new events from server.\", delta.Events.Count);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public void Dispose()\n        {\n            _cts.Cancel();\n            _backgroundTask?.Wait(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));\n            _cts.Dispose();\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.4 Error Handling & Telemetry\n\n* All sync failures are logged via the **crash-reporting** layer.\n* Repeated (5×) failures trip a circuit breaker; UI shows “Unable to Sync.”\n* A/B tests control back-off intervals via the analytics layer.\n\n## 5. Consequences\n\nPositive:\n\n* Seamless offline play; users rarely notice connectivity transitions.\n* Deterministic state ensures fair competitive gameplay.\n* Full audit trail simplifies cheat detection and rollback capabilities.\n\nNegative:\n\n* Event log may bloat; mitigation requires periodic compression.\n* Increased implementation complexity versus naïve approaches.\n* Requires rigorously defined event schema versioning.\n\n## 6. Alternatives Considered\n\n| Alternative | Why Rejected |\n|-------------|--------------|\n| **Last-Write-Wins** keyed by `UpdatedAt` | Prone to economic exploits where older device overwrites modern state. |\n| **Operational Transformation** | Over-engineered for mostly append-only financial events. |\n| **Direct SQLite Replication** | Platform limitations (iOS F_FULLFSYNC) and security constraints. |\n\n## 7. References\n\n* “Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types.” Shapiro et al. _SSS 2011._\n* _Domain-Driven Design_ — Eric Evans.\n* Internal Tech Spec `SYNC-01` for vector clock format.\n\n---\n```","numLines":299,"startLine":1,"totalLines":299}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AbPaE749Ww3T9dVrc4w2oB","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# ADR-001: Layered Architecture\n     3→\n     4→*Status*          | **Accepted**\n     5→------------------|---------------\n     6→*Date*            | 2024-06-01\n     7→*Decision Makers* | @core-arch, @lead-gameplay, @infra-ops\n     8→*Tech Stack*      | C# 11, Unity 2023 LTS, SQLite, Azure App Services\n     9→*Review Cycle*    | Evaluate every major version (v1.0, v2.0, …)\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## 1. Context & Problem Statement  \n    14→\n    15→TycoonVerse is both a **mobile game** and a **financial simulation platform**.  \n    16→We must:\n    17→\n    18→1. Evolve rules (tax, IPO, ESG) without recompiling UI.\n    19→2. Support **offline-first** and later sync to cloud.\n    20→3. Integrate regulated features (biometric wallet, IAP) while staying compliant.\n    21→4. Enable **A/B experimentation** at feature level (analytics, monetization).\n    22→\n    23→A monolithic code-base couples Unity views to business rules, making changes brittle and increasing risk for exploits/cheats. We need a structure that enforces **separation of concerns, testability, and mod-ability**.\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## 2. Decision\n    28→\n    29→Adopt a **4-Layered, Hexagon-inspired** architecture:\n    30→\n    31→```\n    32→┌─────────────────────────────┐\n    33→│        Presentation         │ ← Unity, UI Toolkit\n    34→├─────────┬─────────┬─────────┤\n    35→│Application|Domain |Scripting│ ← Use-cases, Domain Model, Lua modding\n    36→├─────────┴─────────┴─────────┤\n    37→│       Infrastructure        │ ← SQLite, REST, Push, Telemetry\n    38→└─────────────────────────────┘\n    39→```\n    40→\n    41→Key rules:\n    42→\n    43→1. Lower layers **never reference** upper ones (Dependency Inversion).\n    44→2. Cross-layer comms via **contracts** (interfaces, DTOs, events).\n    45→3. **Unity** hosts only Presentation; gameplay data never touches `MonoBehaviour`.\n    46→4. **Domain layer is pure C#** (no UnityEngine), enabling deterministic tests.\n    47→5. **Infrastructure** implements ports (repositories, gateways) and is swapped for mocks in tests.\n    48→\n    49→---\n    50→\n    51→## 3. Drivers\n    52→\n    53→* Deterministic offline simulation (seedable RNG, rollback).\n    54→* App-store compliance: update binary rarely, deliver content via data.\n    55→* Hot-patch economy parameters from back-end without breaking saves.\n    56→* Reduce start-up time by lazy-loading Infrastructure.\n    57→\n    58→---\n    59→\n    60→## 4. Considered Options\n    61→\n    62→Option                             | Description                                   | Notes\n    63→----------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------|------\n    64→1. Tight Unity MVC                | Everything in scenes                          | Fast prototype, poor scaling\n    65→2. ECS-only (DOTS)                | High perf, learning curve                     | Not stable on iOS/Android yet\n    66→3. **Layered / Hexagonal** (✔)    | Ports-and-Adapters, DTOs, Domain events       | Best balance\n    67→\n    68→---\n    69→\n    70→## 5. Decision Outcome\n    71→\n    72→We select **Option 3**.  \n    73→All new packages follow this folder/asmdef layout:\n    74→\n    75→```\n    76→TycoonVerse\n    77→ ├─ Presentation/        (Unity asmdef: TycoonVerse.Presentation)\n    78→ ├─ Application/         (TycoonVerse.Application)\n    79→ ├─ Domain/              (TycoonVerse.Domain)\n    80→ └─ Infrastructure/\n    81→     ├─ Local/           (TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Local)\n    82→     ├─ Remote/          (TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Remote)\n    83→     └─ … \n    84→```\n    85→\n    86→Cross-package dependencies:\n    87→\n    88→```\n    89→Presentation → Application → Domain\n    90→Infrastructure → Domain\n    91→```\n    92→\n    93→---\n    94→\n    95→## 6. Example Code Contracts\n    96→\n    97→### 6.1 Domain\n    98→\n    99→```csharp\n   100→namespace TycoonVerse.Domain.Finance\n   101→{\n   102→    public sealed record Money(decimal Amount, string Currency);\n   103→\n   104→    public interface IClock\n   105→    {\n   106→        DateTime UtcNow { get; }\n   107→    }\n   108→\n   109→    public class CashLedger\n   110→    {\n   111→        private readonly IClock _clock;\n   112→        private readonly List<LedgerEntry> _entries = new();\n   113→\n   114→        public CashLedger(IClock clock) => _clock = clock;\n   115→\n   116→        public void Debit(Money amount, string reason)\n   117→        {\n   118→            if (amount.Amount <= 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(amount));\n   119→            _entries.Add(new LedgerEntry(-amount.Amount, reason, _clock.UtcNow));\n   120→        }\n   121→\n   122→        // …\n   123→    }\n   124→\n   125→    internal record LedgerEntry(decimal Delta, string Reason, DateTime Timestamp);\n   126→}\n   127→```\n   128→\n   129→### 6.2 Application (Use-Case)\n   130→\n   131→```csharp\n   132→namespace TycoonVerse.Application.Finance.Commands\n   133→{\n   134→    public record PurchaseInventory(Guid CompanyId, Money Amount, string Description);\n   135→\n   136→    public class PurchaseInventoryHandler\n   137→    {\n   138→        private readonly ICompanyRepository _repo;\n   139→        private readonly IClock _clock;\n   140→        private readonly IEventBus _bus;\n   141→\n   142→        public PurchaseInventoryHandler(ICompanyRepository repo, IClock clock, IEventBus bus)\n   143→            => (_repo, _clock, _bus) = (repo, clock, bus);\n   144→\n   145→        public async Task HandleAsync(PurchaseInventory cmd, CancellationToken token)\n   146→        {\n   147→            var company = await _repo.LoadAsync(cmd.CompanyId, token);\n   148→            company.Ledger.Debit(cmd.Amount, cmd.Description);\n   149→\n   150→            await _repo.SaveAsync(company, token);\n   151→            await _bus.PublishAsync(new InventoryPurchased(cmd.CompanyId, cmd.Amount, _clock.UtcNow), token);\n   152→        }\n   153→    }\n   154→}\n   155→```\n   156→\n   157→### 6.3 Infrastructure (SQLite Adapter)\n   158→\n   159→```csharp\n   160→namespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Local.Repositories\n   161→{\n   162→    internal sealed class SqliteCompanyRepository : ICompanyRepository\n   163→    {\n   164→        private readonly SqliteConnection _conn;\n   165→        private readonly JsonSerializerOptions _jsonOptions = new() { WriteIndented = false };\n   166→\n   167→        public SqliteCompanyRepository(string dbPath)\n   168→        {\n   169→            _conn = new SqliteConnection($\"Data Source={dbPath}\");\n   170→            _conn.Open();\n   171→        }\n   172→\n   173→        public async Task<Company> LoadAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)\n   174→        {\n   175→            const string sql = \"SELECT payload FROM companies WHERE id = @id\";\n   176→            await using var cmd = _conn.CreateCommand();\n   177→            cmd.CommandText = sql;\n   178→            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue(\"@id\", id.ToString());\n   179→\n   180→            var payload = (string?)await cmd.ExecuteScalarAsync(ct);\n   181→            if (payload is null)\n   182→                throw new DataException($\"Company {id} not found.\");\n   183→\n   184→            return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Company>(payload, _jsonOptions)\n   185→                   ?? throw new DataException(\"Deserialization failed.\");\n   186→        }\n   187→\n   188→        public async Task SaveAsync(Company company, CancellationToken ct)\n   189→        {\n   190→            const string upsert = \"\"\"\n   191→            INSERT INTO companies (id, payload) VALUES (@id, @payload)\n   192→            ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET payload = @payload;\n   193→            \"\"\";\n   194→\n   195→            var json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(company, _jsonOptions);\n   196→            await using var cmd = _conn.CreateCommand();\n   197→            cmd.CommandText = upsert;\n   198→            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue(\"@id\", company.Id.ToString());\n   199→            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue(\"@payload\", json);\n   200→            await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(ct);\n   201→        }\n   202→    }\n   203→}\n   204→```\n   205→\n   206→---\n   207→\n   208→## 7. Consequences\n   209→\n   210→Positive:\n   211→* Unit tests run without Unity runtime (headless CI).\n   212→* Mod-ders can script against Domain DTOs safely.\n   213→* Hot-swap Infrastructure (e.g., switch from SQLite to Mongo) with minimal code change.\n   214→\n   215→Negative:\n   216→* More boilerplate (DTOs, mappers).\n   217→* Steeper onboarding for typical game devs.\n   218→\n   219→---\n   220→\n   221→## 8. Future Work\n   222→\n   223→1. Introduce **Mediator** for decoupled command routing.\n   224→2. Evaluate IL weaving to auto-generate mapping code.\n   225→3. Package Domain as *NuGet* to enable external simulation servers.\n   226→\n   227→---\n   228→\n   229→## 9. References\n   230→\n   231→* [Alistair Cockburn, Hexagonal Architecture](https://alistair.cockburn.us/hexagonal-architecture/)\n   232→* [Unity Assembly Definition Guidelines](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/ScriptCompilationAssemblyDefinitionFiles.html)\n   233→* ADR format inspired by [Michael Nygard](http://thinkrelevance.com/blog/2011/11/15/documenting-architecture-decisions)\n   234→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"52b95c45-8169-4e21-826d-99ed2f9dc283","uuid":"e11eff1c-9e9d-4f88-aa1a-2e8200e0f5a4","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/project/docs/design/ADR-001-Layered-Architecture.md","content":"```markdown\n# ADR-001: Layered Architecture\n\n*Status*          | **Accepted**\n------------------|---------------\n*Date*            | 2024-06-01\n*Decision Makers* | @core-arch, @lead-gameplay, @infra-ops\n*Tech Stack*      | C# 11, Unity 2023 LTS, SQLite, Azure App Services\n*Review Cycle*    | Evaluate every major version (v1.0, v2.0, …)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Context & Problem Statement  \n\nTycoonVerse is both a **mobile game** and a **financial simulation platform**.  \nWe must:\n\n1. Evolve rules (tax, IPO, ESG) without recompiling UI.\n2. Support **offline-first** and later sync to cloud.\n3. Integrate regulated features (biometric wallet, IAP) while staying compliant.\n4. Enable **A/B experimentation** at feature level (analytics, monetization).\n\nA monolithic code-base couples Unity views to business rules, making changes brittle and increasing risk for exploits/cheats. We need a structure that enforces **separation of concerns, testability, and mod-ability**.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Decision\n\nAdopt a **4-Layered, Hexagon-inspired** architecture:\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────┐\n│        Presentation         │ ← Unity, UI Toolkit\n├─────────┬─────────┬─────────┤\n│Application|Domain |Scripting│ ← Use-cases, Domain Model, Lua modding\n├─────────┴─────────┴─────────┤\n│       Infrastructure        │ ← SQLite, REST, Push, Telemetry\n└─────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nKey rules:\n\n1. Lower layers **never reference** upper ones (Dependency Inversion).\n2. Cross-layer comms via **contracts** (interfaces, DTOs, events).\n3. **Unity** hosts only Presentation; gameplay data never touches `MonoBehaviour`.\n4. **Domain layer is pure C#** (no UnityEngine), enabling deterministic tests.\n5. **Infrastructure** implements ports (repositories, gateways) and is swapped for mocks in tests.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Drivers\n\n* Deterministic offline simulation (seedable RNG, rollback).\n* App-store compliance: update binary rarely, deliver content via data.\n* Hot-patch economy parameters from back-end without breaking saves.\n* Reduce start-up time by lazy-loading Infrastructure.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Considered Options\n\nOption                             | Description                                   | Notes\n----------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------|------\n1. Tight Unity MVC                | Everything in scenes                          | Fast prototype, poor scaling\n2. ECS-only (DOTS)                | High perf, learning curve                     | Not stable on iOS/Android yet\n3. **Layered / Hexagonal** (✔)    | Ports-and-Adapters, DTOs, Domain events       | Best balance\n\n---\n\n## 5. Decision Outcome\n\nWe select **Option 3**.  \nAll new packages follow this folder/asmdef layout:\n\n```\nTycoonVerse\n ├─ Presentation/        (Unity asmdef: TycoonVerse.Presentation)\n ├─ Application/         (TycoonVerse.Application)\n ├─ Domain/              (TycoonVerse.Domain)\n └─ Infrastructure/\n     ├─ Local/           (TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Local)\n     ├─ Remote/          (TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Remote)\n     └─ … \n```\n\nCross-package dependencies:\n\n```\nPresentation → Application → Domain\nInfrastructure → Domain\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Example Code Contracts\n\n### 6.1 Domain\n\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Domain.Finance\n{\n    public sealed record Money(decimal Amount, string Currency);\n\n    public interface IClock\n    {\n        DateTime UtcNow { get; }\n    }\n\n    public class CashLedger\n    {\n        private readonly IClock _clock;\n        private readonly List<LedgerEntry> _entries = new();\n\n        public CashLedger(IClock clock) => _clock = clock;\n\n        public void Debit(Money amount, string reason)\n        {\n            if (amount.Amount <= 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(amount));\n            _entries.Add(new LedgerEntry(-amount.Amount, reason, _clock.UtcNow));\n        }\n\n        // …\n    }\n\n    internal record LedgerEntry(decimal Delta, string Reason, DateTime Timestamp);\n}\n```\n\n### 6.2 Application (Use-Case)\n\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Finance.Commands\n{\n    public record PurchaseInventory(Guid CompanyId, Money Amount, string Description);\n\n    public class PurchaseInventoryHandler\n    {\n        private readonly ICompanyRepository _repo;\n        private readonly IClock _clock;\n        private readonly IEventBus _bus;\n\n        public PurchaseInventoryHandler(ICompanyRepository repo, IClock clock, IEventBus bus)\n            => (_repo, _clock, _bus) = (repo, clock, bus);\n\n        public async Task HandleAsync(PurchaseInventory cmd, CancellationToken token)\n        {\n            var company = await _repo.LoadAsync(cmd.CompanyId, token);\n            company.Ledger.Debit(cmd.Amount, cmd.Description);\n\n            await _repo.SaveAsync(company, token);\n            await _bus.PublishAsync(new InventoryPurchased(cmd.CompanyId, cmd.Amount, _clock.UtcNow), token);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 6.3 Infrastructure (SQLite Adapter)\n\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Local.Repositories\n{\n    internal sealed class SqliteCompanyRepository : ICompanyRepository\n    {\n        private readonly SqliteConnection _conn;\n        private readonly JsonSerializerOptions _jsonOptions = new() { WriteIndented = false };\n\n        public SqliteCompanyRepository(string dbPath)\n        {\n            _conn = new SqliteConnection($\"Data Source={dbPath}\");\n            _conn.Open();\n        }\n\n        public async Task<Company> LoadAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            const string sql = \"SELECT payload FROM companies WHERE id = @id\";\n            await using var cmd = _conn.CreateCommand();\n            cmd.CommandText = sql;\n            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue(\"@id\", id.ToString());\n\n            var payload = (string?)await cmd.ExecuteScalarAsync(ct);\n            if (payload is null)\n                throw new DataException($\"Company {id} not found.\");\n\n            return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Company>(payload, _jsonOptions)\n                   ?? throw new DataException(\"Deserialization failed.\");\n        }\n\n        public async Task SaveAsync(Company company, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            const string upsert = \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO companies (id, payload) VALUES (@id, @payload)\n            ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET payload = @payload;\n            \"\"\";\n\n            var json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(company, _jsonOptions);\n            await using var cmd = _conn.CreateCommand();\n            cmd.CommandText = upsert;\n            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue(\"@id\", company.Id.ToString());\n            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue(\"@payload\", json);\n            await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(ct);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Consequences\n\nPositive:\n* Unit tests run without Unity runtime (headless CI).\n* Mod-ders can script against Domain DTOs safely.\n* Hot-swap Infrastructure (e.g., switch from SQLite to Mongo) with minimal code change.\n\nNegative:\n* More boilerplate (DTOs, mappers).\n* Steeper onboarding for typical game devs.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Future Work\n\n1. Introduce **Mediator** for decoupled command routing.\n2. Evaluate IL weaving to auto-generate mapping code.\n3. Package Domain as *NuGet* to enable external simulation servers.\n\n---\n\n## 9. References\n\n* [Alistair Cockburn, Hexagonal Architecture](https://alistair.cockburn.us/hexagonal-architecture/)\n* [Unity Assembly Definition Guidelines](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/ScriptCompilationAssemblyDefinitionFiles.html)\n* ADR format inspired by [Michael Nygard](http://thinkrelevance.com/blog/2011/11/15/documenting-architecture-decisions)\n```","numLines":234,"startLine":1,"totalLines":234}}}

Architectural Overview\n    20→\n    21→```mermaid\n    22→flowchart LR\n    23→    subgraph Presentation [Unity Front-End (MVVM)]\n    24→        UI[3-D Dashboards / HUD]\n    25→        VM[View-Models (C#)]\n    26→    end\n    27→    subgraph Application [Application Layer]\n    28→        CQRS[Commands · Queries · Orchestrators]\n    29→    end\n    30→    subgraph Domain [Domain Core]\n    31→        Entities\n    32→        VOs(Value Objects)\n    33→        DomainServices\n    34→        DomainEvents\n    35→    end\n    36→    subgraph Infrastructure\n    37→        Db[(SQLite)]\n    38→        Net[API Gateway Adapter]\n    39→        Device[Platform Services\\n(Biometrics · Camera · GPS)]\n    40→        Telemetry[(Analytics Sink)]\n    41→    end\n    42→    UI --> VM --> CQRS\n    43→    CQRS -->|Repository Pattern| Domain\n    44→    Domain -- DomainEvents --> CQRS\n    45→    CQRS -->|UnitOfWork| Db\n    46→    CQRS --> Net\n    47→    VM -->|Observer Pattern| Telemetry\n    48→    Device --> VM\n    49→    style Domain fill:#fffbe6,stroke:#cba600,stroke-width:2px\n    50→```\n    51→\n    52→Core tenets:\n    53→\n    54→1. **Strict separation of concerns** across four layers.  \n    55→2. **Domain-Driven Design (DDD)** vocabulary throughout (`Entity`, `ValueObject`, `AggregateRoot`).  \n    56→3. **Ports & Adapters** (Hexagonal) boundary between *Application* and *Infrastructure*.  \n    57→4. **Offline-first** execution path backed by deterministic event sourcing & conflict resolution.  \n    58→\n    59→---\n    60→\n    61→## 3. Directory Layout\n    62→\n    63→```\n    64→TycoonVerse/\n    65→ ├─ Assets/                                   # Unity scenes / prefabs\n    66→ ├─ Src/\n    67→ │   ├─ TycoonVerse.Domain/                   # Entities, ValueObjects, DomainEvents\n    68→ │   ├─ TycoonVerse.Application/              # CQRS, Use-Cases, DTOs\n    69→ │   ├─ TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/           # Data, Network, Platform adapters\n    70→ │   ├─ TycoonVerse.Presentation/             # MVVM view-models & transient UI helpers\n    71→ │   └─ TycoonVerse.Tests/                    # xUnit + NUnit tests\n    72→ └─ Docs/\n    73→     └─ architecture.md                       # ← You are here\n    74→```\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## 4. Key Patterns & Their Enforcement\n    79→\n    80→| Pattern          | Rationale                                     | Enforced By                              |\n    81→|------------------|-----------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------|\n    82→| Singleton        | Avoid duplicate platform service handles      | `Lazy<T>` + DI container (`ServiceLifeTime.Singleton`) |\n    83→| Observer         | Real-time UI refresh & analytics pipeline     | `ReactiveProperty<T>` (UniRx)           |\n    84→| Repository       | Data persistence abstraction                 | Interface in `TycoonVerse.Domain`       |\n    85→| Factory          | Dynamic creation of game objects / offers     | Static `*Factory` classes               |\n    86→| MVVM             | UI testability & designer-developer workflow  | `ViewModelBase` + Unity binding layer   |\n    87→\n    88→> NOTE: **Static classes** are banned unless flagged `// [StaticAllowed]` in code review.\n    89→\n    90→---\n    91→\n    92→## 5. Sample Code Snippets\n    93→\n    94→### 5.1 Domain Entity (simplified)\n    95→\n    96→```csharp\n    97→namespace TycoonVerse.Domain.Entities\n    98→{\n    99→    /// <summary>\n   100→    /// Aggregate root representing a company owned by the player.\n   101→    /// </summary>\n   102→    public sealed class Company : IAggregateRoot\n   103→    {\n   104→        public Guid Id { get; }\n   105→        public CompanyName Name { get; private set; }\n   106→        public Money Cash { get; private set; }\n   107→\n   108→        private readonly List<IDomainEvent> _domainEvents = new();\n   109→\n   110→        public Company(Guid id, CompanyName name, Money seedCapital)\n   111→        {\n   112→            Id = id;\n   113→            Name = name;\n   114→            Cash = seedCapital;\n   115→        }\n   116→\n   117→        public IReadOnlyCollection<IDomainEvent> DomainEvents => _domainEvents.AsReadOnly();\n   118→\n   119→        public void AcquireInventory(InventoryLot lot)\n   120→        {\n   121→            if (Cash < lot.TotalCost)\n   122→                throw new InvalidOperationException(\"Insufficient funds.\");\n   123→\n   124→            Cash -= lot.TotalCost;\n   125→            _domainEvents.Add(new InventoryPurchasedEvent(Id, lot));\n   126→        }\n   127→\n   128→        public void ClearDomainEvents() => _domainEvents.Clear();\n   129→    }\n   130→}\n   131→```\n   132→\n   133→### 5.2 Repository Adapter (Infrastructure)\n   134→\n   135→```csharp\n   136→namespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Persistence\n   137→{\n   138→    internal sealed class CompanyRepository : ICompanyRepository\n   139→    {\n   140→        private readonly SQLiteAsyncConnection _db;\n   141→\n   142→        public CompanyRepository(SQLiteAsyncConnection db) => _db = db;\n   143→\n   144→        public async Task<Company?> GetByIdAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)\n   145→        {\n   146→            var dto = await _db.Table<CompanyDto>().FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id == id);\n   147→            return dto?.ToDomain();\n   148→        }\n   149→\n   150→        public async Task SaveAsync(Company aggregate, CancellationToken ct)\n   151→        {\n   152→            await _db.InsertOrReplaceAsync(CompanyDto.FromDomain(aggregate));\n   153→        }\n   154→    }\n   155→}\n   156→```\n   157→\n   158→### 5.3 View-Model (MVVM + Observer)\n   159→\n   160→```csharp\n   161→public sealed class CompanyDashboardViewModel : ViewModelBase\n   162→{\n   163→    private readonly ICompanyQueryService _query;\n   164→    private readonly IEventBus _bus;\n   165→\n   166→    public ReactiveProperty<decimal> Cash { get; } = new();\n   167→\n   168→    public CompanyDashboardViewModel(ICompanyQueryService query, IEventBus bus)\n   169→    {\n   170→        _query = query;\n   171→        _bus = bus;\n   172→        _bus.Subscribe<InventoryPurchasedEvent>(OnInventoryPurchased);\n   173→    }\n   174→\n   175→    public override async Task InitializeAsync(Guid companyId, CancellationToken ct)\n   176→    {\n   177→        var dto = await _query.GetSnapshotAsync(companyId, ct);\n   178→        Cash.Value = dto.Cash;\n   179→    }\n   180→\n   181→    private void OnInventoryPurchased(InventoryPurchasedEvent e) =>\n   182→        Cash.Value -= e.Lot.TotalCost.Value;\n   183→}\n   184→```\n   185→\n   186→---\n   187→\n   188→## 6. Sequence Diagram – *Inventory Purchase*\n   189→\n   190→```mermaid\n   191→sequenceDiagram\n   192→    participant UI as Unity UI\n   193→    participant VM as Dashboard VM\n   194→    participant App as Application Service\n   195→    participant Repo as CompanyRepository\n   196→    participant DB as SQLite\n   197→\n   198→    UI->>VM: Player taps “Buy Inventory”\n   199→    VM->>App: CreatePurchaseCommand\n   200→    App->>Repo: Load Company\n   201→    Repo->>DB: SELECT * FROM Company WHERE Id=?\n   202→    DB-->>Repo: Row\n   203→    Repo-->>App: Company Aggregate\n   204→    App->>App: Company.AcquireInventory()\n   205→    App->>Repo: Save Company\n   206→    Repo->>DB: INSERT/UPDATE\n   207→    App->>VM: PurchaseResult\n   208→    VM->>UI: Refresh Cash HUD\n   209→```\n   210→\n   211→---\n   212→\n   213→## 7. Cross-Cutting Concerns\n   214→\n   215→1. **Dependency Injection**  \n   216→   *Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection* bootstraps at runtime.  \n   217→   Each Unity scene receives a scoped container to avoid memory leaks.\n   218→\n   219→2. **Logging & Crash Reporting**  \n   220→   *Serilog* sinks to both local file ring-buffer and *Backtrace* cloud.\n   221→\n   222→3. **Analytics & A/B Testing**  \n   223→   Events are queued locally via LiteDB; flush interval = *15 s* or on scene exit.\n   224→\n   225→4. **Biometric Authentication**  \n   226→   Wrapped in an `IBiometricService` adapter; falls back to PIN if unavailable.\n   227→\n   228→5. **Offline Sync**  \n   229→   Domain events are appended to an *Event Store* (LiteDB).  \n   230→   A background worker replicates to server once `INetworkProbe.IsOnline == true`.\n   231→\n   232→---\n   233→\n   234→## 8. Error-Handling Strategy\n   235→\n   236→| Layer          | Strategy                                               |\n   237→|----------------|--------------------------------------------------------|\n   238→| Domain         | Throw specific exceptions (never swallow).            |\n   239→| Application    | Map domain errors to `Result<T>` objects.             |\n   240→| Presentation   | Display user-friendly toast / dialog via `IUIService`.|\n   241→| Infrastructure | Circuit-breaker (Polly) for network & I/O retries.    |\n   242→\n   243→---\n   244→\n   245→## 9. Performance Targets\n   246→\n   247→| Metric                     | Budget                                  |\n   248→|----------------------------|-----------------------------------------|\n   249→| Cold start (Android)       | ≤ 5 sec on Pixel 4                      |\n   250→| Frame-time (UI Scenes)     | ≤ 16 ms (60 FPS)                        |\n   251→| Save-game size             | ≤ 2 MB                                  |\n   252→| Telemetry overhead         | ≤ 2 % CPU / frame                       |\n   253→\n   254→---\n   255→\n   256→## 10. Extending the Game Loop\n   257→\n   258→Follow these steps to introduce a new feature (ex. **Payroll**):\n   259→\n   260→1. Model aggregates & events under `TycoonVerse.Domain`.  \n   261→2. Define commands/queries & handlers in `TycoonVerse.Application`.  \n   262→3. Implement repository + adapters in `Infrastructure`.  \n   263→4. Expose UI via new Scene or Prefab, bind to a fresh View-Model.  \n   264→5. Add tests at **all** levels; code coverage must not drop below **85 %**.  \n   265→6. Document the change in `CHANGELOG.md`.\n   266→\n   267→---\n   268→\n   269→## 11. Tooling & CI/CD\n   270→\n   271→* GitHub Actions → dotnet build | test | codecov | AddressSanitizer  \n   272→* Unity Batch-mode → *Asset-bundles* & *Android AAB*  \n   273→* Fastlane → TestFlight / Google Play Internal  \n   274→\n   275→---\n   276→\n   277→## 12. Glossary\n   278→\n   279→* **Aggregate Root** – Cluster of domain objects treated as a single unit.  \n   280→* **CQRS** – Command Query Responsibility Segregation.  \n   281→* **Value Object** – Immutable object defined solely by its data.  \n   282→* **Unit of Work** – Transactional batching of repository operations.  \n   283→\n   284→---\n   285→\n   286→© 2024 TycoonVerse Studios. All Rights Reserved.\n   287→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Purpose\n\nThis document serves as the canonical description of TycoonVerse’s high-level and low-level architecture.  \nIt explains *how* and *why* the codebase is structured, which patterns are enforced, and where future\ncontributors should plug in new functionality without introducing **tech-debt** or **regression risk**.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Architectural Overview\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Presentation [Unity Front-End (MVVM)]\n        UI[3-D Dashboards / HUD]\n        VM[View-Models (C#)]\n    end\n    subgraph Application [Application Layer]\n        CQRS[Commands · Queries · Orchestrators]\n    end\n    subgraph Domain [Domain Core]\n        Entities\n        VOs(Value Objects)\n        DomainServices\n        DomainEvents\n    end\n    subgraph Infrastructure\n        Db[(SQLite)]\n        Net[API Gateway Adapter]\n        Device[Platform Services\\n(Biometrics · Camera · GPS)]\n        Telemetry[(Analytics Sink)]\n    end\n    UI --> VM --> CQRS\n    CQRS -->|Repository Pattern| Domain\n    Domain -- DomainEvents --> CQRS\n    CQRS -->|UnitOfWork| Db\n    CQRS --> Net\n    VM -->|Observer Pattern| Telemetry\n    Device --> VM\n    style Domain fill:#fffbe6,stroke:#cba600,stroke-width:2px\n```\n\nCore tenets:\n\n1. **Strict separation of concerns** across four layers.  \n2. **Domain-Driven Design (DDD)** vocabulary throughout (`Entity`, `ValueObject`, `AggregateRoot`).  \n3. **Ports & Adapters** (Hexagonal) boundary between *Application* and *Infrastructure*.  \n4. **Offline-first** execution path backed by deterministic event sourcing & conflict resolution.  \n\n---\n\n## 3. Key Patterns & Their Enforcement\n\n| Pattern          | Rationale                                     | Enforced By                              |\n|------------------|-----------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------|\n| Singleton        | Avoid duplicate platform service handles      | `Lazy<T>` + DI container (`ServiceLifeTime.Singleton`) |\n| Observer         | Real-time UI refresh & analytics pipeline     | `ReactiveProperty<T>` (UniRx)           |\n| Repository       | Data persistence abstraction                 | Interface in `TycoonVerse.Domain`       |\n| Factory          | Dynamic creation of game objects / offers     | Static `*Factory` classes               |\n| MVVM             | UI testability & designer-developer workflow  | `ViewModelBase` + Unity binding layer   |\n\n> NOTE: **Static classes** are banned unless flagged `// [StaticAllowed]` in code review.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Sample Code Snippets\n\n### 5.1 Domain Entity (simplified)\n\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Domain.Entities\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Aggregate root representing a company owned by the player.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class Company : IAggregateRoot\n    {\n        public Guid Id { get; }\n        public CompanyName Name { get; private set; }\n        public Money Cash { get; private set; }\n\n        private readonly List<IDomainEvent> _domainEvents = new();\n\n        public Company(Guid id, CompanyName name, Money seedCapital)\n        {\n            Id = id;\n            Name = name;\n            Cash = seedCapital;\n        }\n\n        public IReadOnlyCollection<IDomainEvent> DomainEvents => _domainEvents.AsReadOnly();\n\n        public void AcquireInventory(InventoryLot lot)\n        {\n            if (Cash < lot.TotalCost)\n                throw new InvalidOperationException(\"Insufficient funds.\");\n\n            Cash -= lot.TotalCost;\n            _domainEvents.Add(new InventoryPurchasedEvent(Id, lot));\n        }\n\n        public void ClearDomainEvents() => _domainEvents.Clear();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 Repository Adapter (Infrastructure)\n\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Persistence\n{\n    internal sealed class CompanyRepository : ICompanyRepository\n    {\n        private readonly SQLiteAsyncConnection _db;\n\n        public CompanyRepository(SQLiteAsyncConnection db) => _db = db;\n\n        public async Task<Company?> GetByIdAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var dto = await _db.Table<CompanyDto>().FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id == id);\n            return dto?.ToDomain();\n        }\n\n        public async Task SaveAsync(Company aggregate, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            await _db.InsertOrReplaceAsync(CompanyDto.FromDomain(aggregate));\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 5.3 View-Model (MVVM + Observer)\n\n```csharp\npublic sealed class CompanyDashboardViewModel : ViewModelBase\n{\n    private readonly ICompanyQueryService _query;\n    private readonly IEventBus _bus;\n\n    public ReactiveProperty<decimal> Cash { get; } = new();\n\n    public CompanyDashboardViewModel(ICompanyQueryService query, IEventBus bus)\n    {\n        _query = query;\n        _bus = bus;\n        _bus.Subscribe<InventoryPurchasedEvent>(OnInventoryPurchased);\n    }\n\n    public override async Task InitializeAsync(Guid companyId, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var dto = await _query.GetSnapshotAsync(companyId, ct);\n        Cash.Value = dto.Cash;\n    }\n\n    private void OnInventoryPurchased(InventoryPurchasedEvent e) =>\n        Cash.Value -= e.Lot.TotalCost.Value;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Sequence Diagram – *Inventory Purchase*\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant UI as Unity UI\n    participant VM as Dashboard VM\n    participant App as Application Service\n    participant Repo as CompanyRepository\n    participant DB as SQLite\n\n    UI->>VM: Player taps “Buy Inventory”\n    VM->>App: CreatePurchaseCommand\n    App->>Repo: Load Company\n    Repo->>DB: SELECT * FROM Company WHERE Id=?\n    DB-->>Repo: Row\n    Repo-->>App: Company Aggregate\n    App->>App: Company.AcquireInventory()\n    App->>Repo: Save Company\n    Repo->>DB: INSERT/UPDATE\n    App->>VM: PurchaseResult\n    VM->>UI: Refresh Cash HUD\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n1. **Dependency Injection**  \n   *Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection* bootstraps at runtime.  \n   Each Unity scene receives a scoped container to avoid memory leaks.\n\n2. **Logging & Crash Reporting**  \n   *Serilog* sinks to both local file ring-buffer and *Backtrace* cloud.\n\n3. **Analytics & A/B Testing**  \n   Events are queued locally via LiteDB; flush interval = *15 s* or on scene exit.\n\n4. **Biometric Authentication**  \n   Wrapped in an `IBiometricService` adapter; falls back to PIN if unavailable.\n\n5. **Offline Sync**  \n   Domain events are appended to an *Event Store* (LiteDB).  \n   A background worker replicates to server once `INetworkProbe.IsOnline == true`.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Error-Handling Strategy\n\n| Layer          | Strategy                                               |\n|----------------|--------------------------------------------------------|\n| Domain         | Throw specific exceptions (never swallow).            |\n| Application    | Map domain errors to `Result<T>` objects.             |\n| Presentation   | Display user-friendly toast / dialog via `IUIService`.|\n| Infrastructure | Circuit-breaker (Polly) for network & I/O retries.    |\n\n---\n\n## 9. Performance Targets\n\n| Metric                     | Budget                                  |\n|----------------------------|-----------------------------------------|\n| Cold start (Android)       | ≤ 5 sec on Pixel 4                      |\n| Frame-time (UI Scenes)     | ≤ 16 ms (60 FPS)                        |\n| Save-game size             | ≤ 2 MB                                  |\n| Telemetry overhead         | ≤ 2 % CPU / frame                       |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Extending the Game Loop\n\nFollow these steps to introduce a new feature (ex. **Payroll**):\n\n1. Model aggregates & events under `TycoonVerse.Domain`.  \n2. Define commands/queries & handlers in `TycoonVerse.Application`.  \n3. Implement repository + adapters in `Infrastructure`.  \n4. Expose UI via new Scene or Prefab, bind to a fresh View-Model.  \n5. Add tests at **all** levels; code coverage must not drop below **85 %**.  \n6. Document the change in `CHANGELOG.md`.\n\n---\n\n## 11. Tooling & CI/CD\n\n* GitHub Actions → dotnet build | test | codecov | AddressSanitizer  \n* Unity Batch-mode → *Asset-bundles* & *Android AAB*  \n* Fastlane → TestFlight / Google Play Internal  \n\n---\n\n## 12. Glossary\n\n* **Aggregate Root** – Cluster of domain objects treated as a single unit.  \n* **CQRS** – Command Query Responsibility Segregation.  \n* **Value Object** – Immutable object defined solely by its data.  \n* **Unit of Work** – Transactional batching of repository operations.  \n\n---\n\n© 2024 TycoonVerse Studios. All Rights Reserved.\n```","numLines":287,"startLine":1,"totalLines":287}}}

.ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                dispatched += batchList.Count;\n                confirmed += response.ConfirmedCount;\n\n                // Mark confirmed actions as synced in local Will retry later.\");\n\n                // Any exception aborts the current batch but keeps the loop running,\n                // maximizing the amount of data we can still sync.\n                // No additional work needed; the unsynced actions remain pending.\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Emit high-level analytic summary\n        _analytics.TrackEvent(\"player_action_sync_summary\", new\n        {\n            dispatched,\n            confirmed,\n            failed\n        });\n\n        return new SyncPlayerActionsResult(dispatched, confirmed, failed);\n    }\n}\n\n#region Supporting Abstractions (contracts)\n\n/// <summary>\n///     Contract for a repository that stores player actions locally (SQLite).\n/// </summary>\npublic interface IPlayerActionRepository\n{\n    Task<IReadOnlyList<PlayerAction>> GetPendingAsync(bool orderByTimestampAsc, CancellationToken token);\n    Task MarkAsSyncedAsync(IEnumerable<Guid> actionIds, CancellationToken token);\n    Task MarkAsFailedAsync(IEnumerable<PlayerAction> actions, CancellationToken token);\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n///     Contract for a service responsible for pushing local mutations to the\n///     authoritative back-end service.\n/// </summary>\npublic interface ISyncService\n{\n    Task<SyncResponse> PushAsync(IReadOnlyCollection<PlayerAction> actions, CancellationToken token);\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n///     Response returned by <see cref=\"ISyncService\"/> after a push attempt.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class SyncResponse\n{\n    public SyncResponse(\n        IReadOnlyCollection<Guid> confirmedIds,\n        IReadOnlyCollection<PlayerAction> rejectedItems,\n        TimeSpan latency)\n    {\n        ConfirmedIds = confirmedIds;\n        RejectedItems = rejectedItems;\n        Latency = latency;\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>IDs of actions that the server accepted and persisted.</summary>\n    public IReadOnlyCollection<Guid> ConfirmedIds { get; positive.\");\n\n            lock (_sync)\n            {\n                var today = clock.UtcNow.Date;\n                var debitedToday = _dailyDebits.TryGetValue(today, out var total) ? total : _walletBalance, -amount);\n                _dailyDebits[today] = debitedToday + amount;\n            }\n\n            Publish(new WalletDebitedEvent(Id, amount, WalletBalance, reason));\n        }\n\n Publish(PlayerDomainEvent @event)\n        {\n            // Defensive copy avoids concurrency woes.\n            var snapshot = _observers.ToArray();\n            foreach (var observer in snapshot)\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    observer.OnNext(@event);\n                }\n                catch (Exception e)\n                {\n                    //      {\n            private readonly IList<IObserver<PlayerDomainEvent>> _list;\n            private readonly IObserver<PlayerDomainEvent> _observer;\n\n            public Unsubscriber(IList<IObserver<PlayerDomainEvent>> list, IObserver<PlayerDomainEvent> observer)\n            {\n                _list = list;\n                _observer = observer;\n            }\n\n            public void Dispose()\n            {\n                if (_list.Contains(_observer))\n                    _list.Remove(_observer);\n            }\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n\n  {\n        _playerRepository = playerRepository;\n        _passwordService = passwordService;\n        _biometricAuthService = biometricAuthService;\n        _tokenService = tokenService;\n        _offlineCache = offlineCache;\n        _analytics = analytics;\n        _dateTime = dateTime;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<AuthenticatePlayerResponse> Handle(\n        AuthenticatePlayerCommand command,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // 1. Sanity validation -------------------------------------------------------------------\n        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(command.Email))\n            return AuthenticatePlayerResponse.Fail(\"Email must be provided.\");\n\n        // 2. Attempt offline authentication if requested ----------------------------------------\n        if (command.OfflineModeRequested)\n        {\n            var cached = _offlineCache.TryGet(command.Email);\n            if (cached != null)\n            {\n                _analytics.TrackEvent(AnalyticsEvent.OfflineAuthGranted, command.Email);\n                return AuthenticatePlayerResponse.Offline(cached);\n            }\n\n            // If offline login was explicitly requested and no cache found, we *fail fast*\n            return AuthenticatePlayerResponse.Fail(\"Offline credentials not found.\");\n        }\n\n        // 3. Fetch player aggregate from repository ---------------------------------------------\n        var player = await _playerRepository.GetByEmailAsync(command.Email, cancellationToken);\n        if (player is null)\n            return AuthenticatePlayerResponse.Fail(\"Player not found.\");\n\n        // 4. Validate credentials ----------------------------------------------------------------\n        bool isCredentialValid = false;\n\n        // 4a. Password flow\n        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(command.Password))\n        {\n            isCredentialValid = _passwordService.VerifyHashedPassword(\n                player.PasswordHash,\n                command.Password);\n        }\n\n        // 4b. Biometric flow (if password failed / not provided)\n        if (!isCredentialValid && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(command.BiometricSignature))\n        {\n            isCredentialValid = await _biometricAuthService.ValidateAsync(\n                player.Id,\n                command.BiometricSignature,\n                cancellationToken);\n        }\n\n        if (!isCredentialValid)\n        {\n            _analytics.TrackEvent(AnalyticsEvent.AuthFailed, command.Email);\n            return AuthenticatePlayerResponse.Fail(\"Invalid credentials.\");\n        }\n\n        // 5. Build JWT and persist refresh token -------------------------------------------------\n        var (jwt, expiresAt) = _tokenService.GenerateAccessToken(player);\n        player.RefreshToken = _tokenService.GenerateRefreshToken();\n        player.RefreshTokenExpiry = expiresAt.AddDays(7);\n\n        await _playerRepository.UpdateAsync(player, cancellationToken);\n\n        // 6. Cache profile for possible offline authentication -----------------------------------\n        var profileDto = PlayerProfileDto.From(player);\n        _offlineCache.Set(command.Email, profileDto);\n\n        // 7. Track analytics ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        _analytics.TrackEvent(AnalyticsEvent.AuthSucceeded, command.Email);\n\n        // 8. Return success ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n        return AuthenticatePlayerResponse.Success(profileDto, jwt, expiresAt);\n    }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n//  Interfaces required by this feature.  They are implemented in other layers.\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Common.Interfaces\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Repository abstraction for <see cref=\"Player\"/> aggregates.\n    /// </summary>\n    public interface IPlayerRepository\n    {\n        Task<Player?> GetByEmailAsync(string email, CancellationToken ct);\n        Task UpdateAsync(Player player, CancellationToken ct);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Responsible for hashing and verifying player passwords.</summary>\n    public interface IPasswordService\n    {\n        bool VerifyHashedPassword(string hashedPassword, string providedPassword);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Service that delegates biometric verification to platform APIs.</summary>\n    public interface IBiometricAuthService\n    {\n        Task<bool> ValidateAsync(PlayerId playerId, string biometricSignature, CancellationToken ct);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Generates access & refresh tokens following the OAuth 2.0 spec.</summary>\n    public interface ITokenService\n    {\n        (string Jwt, DateTimeOffset ExpiresAt) GenerateAccessToken(Player player);\n        string GenerateRefreshToken();\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Local cache holding last-known good credentials to unlock offline play.\n    /// </summary>\n    public interface IOfflineAuthCache\n    {\n        PlayerProfileDto? TryGet(string email);\n        void Set(string email, PlayerProfileDto profile);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Thin wrapper around analytics SDK for feature-level events.</summary>\n    public interface IAnalyticsService\n    {\n        void TrackEvent(string @event, string playerIdentifier);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///     Provides <see cref=\"DateTimeOffset.UtcNow\"/> access for testability.\n    /// </summary>\n    public interface IDateTimeProvider\n    {\n        DateTimeOffset UtcNow { get; }\n    }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n//  DTOs & shared constants\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Common.Models\n{\n    /// <summary>Lightweight representation of a player's public profile.</summary>\n    public sealed class PlayerProfileDto\n    {\n        public string Id { get; init; } = default!;\n        public string Email { get; init; } = default!;\n        public string DisplayName { get; init; } = default!;\n        public long CashOnHand { get; init; }\n\n        public static PlayerProfileDto From(Player player) => new()\n        {\n            Id = player.Id.Value,\n            Email = player.Email,\n            DisplayName = player.DisplayName,\n            CashOnHand = player.CashOnHand\n        };\n    }\n}\n\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Telemetry\n{\n    public static class AnalyticsEvent\n    {\n        public const string AuthSucceeded = \"auth_success\";\n        public const string AuthFailed = \"auth_failure\";\n        public const string OfflineAuthGranted = \"auth_offline_grant\";\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":301,"startLine":1,"totalLines":301}}}
