{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01Q4mC119aNCGispvDehinUA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NXcvKES5eA5UkiHqjm2PEY","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Architectural Proposal: Real-Time 'Hardcore Mode' Synchronization\n\n**Author:** Lead Architect\n**Date:** February 2026\n**Status:** Proposal\n**Impact:** High - Architectural change affecting sync subsystem\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document proposes an architectural solution to support a new \"Hardcore Mode\" feature that requires real-time server synchronization for competitive players, while preserving the existing offline-first experience for standard players. The solution leverages the **Strategy Pattern** to encapsulate the two synchronization behaviors, maintaining adherence to the Open/Closed Principle and the established layered architecture.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Analysis of Conflict\n\n### 1.1 Current Architecture (ADR-003)\n\nThe TycoonVerse application is fundamentally built on an **offline-first architecture** as documented in `docs/design/ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md`. The key design decisions include:\n\n1. **Client-Side Event Sourcing**: All player actions are captured as domain events and stored locally in SQLite before any server communication occurs (ADR-003, Section 2).\n\n2. **Periodic Background Sync**: The `OfflineSyncManager` (documented in ADR-003:136-261) operates as a singleton that runs a background pump every 15 seconds, batching events and sending them to the server only when connectivity is available.\n\n3. **Queue-First Design**: When actions occur, they are:\n   - First persisted to the local `GameEventLog` SQLite table (ADR-003:99-116)\n   - Marked with a `SyncSessionId` of `NULL` until synced\n   - Eventually pushed via `POST /events/ingest` endpoint\n\n4. **Eventual Consistency**: The system explicitly accepts that client state may diverge from server state temporarily, with CRDT-inspired merge algorithms resolving conflicts later.\n\n### 1.2 Evidence in Source Code\n\nThe offline-first pattern is consistently implemented across the codebase:\n\n**`SyncPlayerActionsCommand.cs:56-69`** - The synchronization is explicitly batch-oriented:\n```csharp\n// Load unsynced actions deterministically ordered by creation date so that\n// the back-end can replay game state in exactly the same way as the client.\nIReadOnlyList<PlayerAction> pending = await _actionRepository\n    .GetPendingAsync(orderByTimestampAsc: true, cancellationToken)\n```\n\n**`ApiClient.cs:97-101`** - Network calls queue locally when offline:\n```csharp\nif (!_connectivity.IsOnline)\n{\n    await QueueOfflineAsync(request, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n    return default;\n}\n```\n\n**`CreateCompanyCommand.cs:107-109`** - Commands persist locally without immediate server sync:\n```csharp\nawait _companyRepository.AddAsync(company, cancellationToken);\nawait _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);\n```\n\n### 1.3 The Fundamental Conflict\n\n**Hardcore Mode Requirement:** All significant player actions must be synchronized with the server in real-time, meaning:\n- The action cannot be considered \"successful\" until the server confirms it\n- Network latency directly impacts gameplay\n- Connectivity is mandatory; actions fail if offline\n\n**Current Architecture Assumption:** Actions succeed locally first, server synchronization happens later, and offline play is fully supported.\n\nThese requirements are mutually exclusive and cannot coexist in the same code path without architectural intervention. Simply adding `if (isHardcoreMode)` checks throughout the codebase would:\n- Violate the **Open/Closed Principle** (classes should be open for extension, closed for modification)\n- Create tight coupling between game mode logic and persistence/sync logic\n- Result in fragile, hard-to-test code with conditional branching in every command handler\n- Make the system resistant to future changes (e.g., adding a third sync mode)\n\n---\n\n## 2. Proposed Architectural Pattern\n\n### 2.1 Primary Pattern: Strategy Pattern\n\nThe **Strategy Pattern** is the optimal solution for this requirement. It allows us to define a family of algorithms (synchronization strategies), encapsulate each one in a separate class, and make them interchangeable at runtime.\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        IActionSyncStrategy                        │\n│  + ExecuteAndSyncAsync(action, ct) : Task<SyncResult>            │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                ▲\n                                │ implements\n                ┌───────────────┴───────────────┐\n                │                               │\n┌───────────────────────────┐   ┌───────────────────────────────────┐\n│  OfflineQueuingStrategy   │   │    RealTimeSyncStrategy           │\n│  - Persists locally first │   │    - Sends to server immediately  │\n│  - Queues for later sync  │   │    - Awaits server confirmation   │\n│  - Returns optimistic OK  │   │    - Fails if offline/rejected    │\n└───────────────────────────┘   └───────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 2.2 Why Strategy Pattern Over Alternatives\n\n| Alternative Approach | Why Rejected |\n|---------------------|--------------|\n| **Naive `if/else` checks** | Violates Open/Closed Principle; scatters game mode logic across entire codebase; makes testing difficult; creates maintenance burden when adding new modes |\n| **Decorator Pattern** | Decorators are better for adding behavior transparently; our strategies have fundamentally different behaviors, not additive enhancements |\n| **Template Method** | Requires inheritance hierarchy; less flexible than composition; harder to switch strategies at runtime |\n| **State Pattern** | Better for state transitions; our need is behavior selection, not state management |\n\n### 2.3 Supporting Pattern: Factory Pattern\n\nA **Sync Strategy Factory** will be responsible for creating and returning the appropriate strategy based on the current player context (game mode). This cleanly separates:\n- **Decision logic** (which strategy to use) - in the factory\n- **Execution logic** (how to sync) - in the strategies\n\n---\n\n## 3. Component Placement and Responsibility\n\n### 3.1 New Components by Layer\n\nFollowing the layered architecture defined in `ADR-001-Layered-Architecture.md`:\n\n#### **Application Layer** (`TycoonVerse.Application`)\n\n| Component | Location | Responsibility |\n|-----------|----------|----------------|\n| `IActionSyncStrategy` | `/Interfaces/Services/IActionSyncStrategy.cs` | Strategy interface defining the sync contract |\n| `ISyncStrategyFactory` | `/Interfaces/Services/ISyncStrategyFactory.cs` | Factory interface for creating strategies |\n| `SyncResult` | `/Common/Models/SyncResult.cs` | Result type returned by sync operations |\n| `PlayerAction` (extended) | `/Features/Sync/PlayerAction.cs` | Already exists; may need minimal extension |\n\n**Interface Definition:**\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Interfaces.Services\n{\n    public interface IActionSyncStrategy\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Executes the given player action and synchronizes it according to\n        /// the strategy's semantics (offline-queued or real-time).\n        /// </summary>\n        Task<SyncResult> ExecuteAndSyncAsync(\n            PlayerAction action,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken);\n    }\n\n    public interface ISyncStrategyFactory\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Returns the appropriate sync strategy based on the player's game mode.\n        /// </summary>\n        IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(Guid playerId);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### **Infrastructure Layer** (`TycoonVerse.Infrastructure`)\n\n| Component | Location | Responsibility |\n|-----------|----------|----------------|\n| `OfflineQueuingStrategy` | `/Services/Sync/OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs` | Implements existing offline-first behavior |\n| `RealTimeSyncStrategy` | `/Services/Sync/RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs` | Implements real-time server sync |\n| `SyncStrategyFactory` | `/Services/Sync/SyncStrategyFactory.cs` | Concrete factory implementation |\n\n#### **Core/Domain Layer** (`TycoonVerse.Core`)\n\n| Component | Location | Responsibility |\n|-----------|----------|----------------|\n| `GameMode` enum | `/Enums/GameMode.cs` | Defines available game modes |\n| `Player.GameMode` property | `/Entities/Player.cs` | Stores player's selected mode |\n\n### 3.2 Existing Classes Requiring Modification\n\nThe following existing classes will need modifications to integrate the new sync strategy:\n\n| File | Modification Required |\n|------|----------------------|\n| `TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs:89-120` | Add `GameMode` property to store player's mode selection |\n| `TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/CreateCompanyCommand.cs:48-121` | Inject `ISyncStrategyFactory`; use strategy after local persistence |\n| `TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/FileIPOCommand.cs:67-162` | Inject `ISyncStrategyFactory`; use strategy after domain operation |\n| `TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Market/GetMarketDataQuery.cs` | Read operations may need real-time data for hardcore mode |\n| `TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Networking/ApiClient.cs:25-278` | Add `SendImmediateAsync()` method for blocking sync calls |\n| `TycoonVerse.Application/Common/ServiceLocator.cs:20-314` | Register new strategy and factory services |\n| `TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Persistence/UnitOfWork.cs` | May need to expose sync hooks for strategy consumption |\n\n### 3.3 Project Structure After Changes\n\n```\nTycoonVerse.Application/\n├── Interfaces/\n│   └── Services/\n│       ├── IActionSyncStrategy.cs      ← NEW\n│       ├── ISyncStrategyFactory.cs     ← NEW\n│       └── IUnitOfWork.cs              (existing)\n├── Common/\n│   ├── Models/\n│   │   └── SyncResult.cs               ← NEW\n│   └── ServiceLocator.cs               (modified)\n└── Features/\n    └── Company/\n        ├── CreateCompanyCommand.cs     (modified)\n        └── FileIPOCommand.cs           (modified)\n\nTycoonVerse.Infrastructure/\n├── Services/\n│   └── Sync/                           ← NEW directory\n│       ├── OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs   ← NEW\n│       ├── RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs     ← NEW\n│       └── SyncStrategyFactory.cs      ← NEW\n└── Networking/\n    └── ApiClient.cs                    (modified)\n\nTycoonVerse.Core/\n├── Entities/\n│   └── Player.cs                       (modified)\n└── Enums/\n    └── GameMode.cs                     ← NEW\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Decision Logic: Where and How\n\n### 4.1 Decision Point Location\n\nThe **`SyncStrategyFactory`** is the single component responsible for determining which synchronization path to use. This follows the **Single Responsibility Principle** - the factory's only job is making this decision.\n\n**Location:** `TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Services/Sync/SyncStrategyFactory.cs`\n\n### 4.2 Decision Flow\n\n```\n┌─────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐\n│  Command        │───>│  SyncStrategyFactory │───>│  IActionSyncStrategy│\n│  Handler        │    │  GetStrategy(playerId)│    │  (concrete impl)    │\n│                 │    └──────────────────────┘    └─────────────────────┘\n└─────────────────┘             │                           │\n                                │                           │\n                                ▼                           ▼\n                    ┌───────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐\n                    │  IPlayerRepository    │    │  ExecuteAndSyncAsync│\n                    │  GetGameMode(playerId)│    │  (action, ct)       │\n                    └───────────────────────┘    └─────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4.3 Decision Algorithm\n\n```csharp\npublic class SyncStrategyFactory : ISyncStrategyFactory\n{\n    private readonly IPlayerRepository _playerRepository;\n    private readonly IConnectivityService _connectivity;\n    private readonly OfflineQueuingStrategy _offlineStrategy;\n    private readonly RealTimeSyncStrategy _realTimeStrategy;\n\n    public IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(Guid playerId)\n    {\n        var player = _playerRepository.GetCached(playerId);\n\n        // Decision logic is centralized HERE\n        if (player?.GameMode == GameMode.Hardcore)\n        {\n            // Real-time mode requires connectivity\n            if (!_connectivity.IsOnline)\n            {\n                throw new HardcoreModeOfflineException(\n                    \"Hardcore Mode requires an active internet connection.\");\n            }\n            return _realTimeStrategy;\n        }\n\n        // Default: standard offline-first behavior\n        return _offlineStrategy;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.4 Integration with Command Handlers\n\nModified command handler pattern (using `CreateCompanyCommand` as example):\n\n```csharp\ninternal sealed class CreateCompanyCommandHandler\n    : IRequestHandler<CreateCompanyCommand, CreateCompanyResult>\n{\n    private readonly ICompanyRepository _companyRepository;\n    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;\n    private readonly ISyncStrategyFactory _syncStrategyFactory;  // NEW\n    private readonly ILogger<CreateCompanyCommandHandler> _logger;\n\n    public async Task<CreateCompanyResult> Handle(\n        CreateCompanyCommand request,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // ... existing validation and domain logic ...\n\n        // Create the domain entity\n        var company = Company.Create(...);\n\n        // Persist locally (unchanged)\n        await _companyRepository.AddAsync(company, cancellationToken);\n        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);\n\n        // NEW: Get sync strategy and execute sync\n        var strategy = _syncStrategyFactory.GetStrategy(request.RequestedByPlayerId);\n        var action = PlayerAction.CreateCompanyAction(company);\n\n        var syncResult = await strategy.ExecuteAndSyncAsync(action, cancellationToken);\n\n        if (!syncResult.IsSuccess && syncResult.RequiresRollback)\n        {\n            // Hardcore mode: server rejected, rollback local changes\n            await _companyRepository.RemoveAsync(company.Id, cancellationToken);\n            await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);\n            throw new ServerRejectionException(syncResult.ErrorMessage);\n        }\n\n        return new CreateCompanyResult(company.Id, company.CreatedAtUtc);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Strategy Implementation Details\n\n### 5.1 OfflineQueuingStrategy (Encapsulates Existing Behavior)\n\nThis strategy encapsulates the current offline-first behavior already documented in ADR-003:\n\n```csharp\npublic class OfflineQueuingStrategy : IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IPlayerActionRepository _actionRepository;\n    private readonly ILogger<OfflineQueuingStrategy> _logger;\n\n    public async Task<SyncResult> ExecuteAndSyncAsync(\n        PlayerAction action,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // Queue for later sync (existing behavior from ADR-003)\n        await _actionRepository.AddPendingAsync(action, cancellationToken);\n\n        _logger.LogDebug(\n            \"Action {ActionId} queued for offline sync.\",\n            action.Id);\n\n        // Always return success - action is persisted locally\n        return SyncResult.QueuedForSync(action.Id);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 RealTimeSyncStrategy (New Behavior)\n\nThis strategy implements the new real-time synchronization requirement:\n\n```csharp\npublic class RealTimeSyncStrategy : IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IApiClient _apiClient;\n    private readonly ILogger<RealTimeSyncStrategy> _logger;\n    private readonly TimeSpan _timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10);\n\n    public async Task<SyncResult> ExecuteAndSyncAsync(\n        PlayerAction action,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        using var cts = CancellationTokenSource\n            .CreateLinkedTokenSource(cancellationToken);\n        cts.CancelAfter(_timeout);\n\n        try\n        {\n            var request = new ApiRequest(\n                new Uri(\"/events/immediate\", UriKind.Relative),\n                HttpMethod.Post,\n                body: action);\n\n            var response = await _apiClient\n                .SendAsync<ImmediateSyncResponse>(request, cts.Token);\n\n            if (response?.Accepted == true)\n            {\n                _logger.LogInformation(\n                    \"Action {ActionId} confirmed by server in real-time.\",\n                    action.Id);\n                return SyncResult.Confirmed(action.Id, response.ServerSequence);\n            }\n\n            _logger.LogWarning(\n                \"Server rejected action {ActionId}: {Reason}\",\n                action.Id,\n                response?.RejectionReason);\n\n            return SyncResult.Rejected(\n                action.Id,\n                response?.RejectionReason ?? \"Unknown server error\",\n                requiresRollback: true);\n        }\n        catch (OperationCanceledException)\n        {\n            _logger.LogError(\"Real-time sync timed out for action {ActionId}\", action.Id);\n            return SyncResult.Failed(action.Id, \"Server response timeout\", requiresRollback: true);\n        }\n        catch (ApiException ex) when (ex.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.Conflict)\n        {\n            return SyncResult.Rejected(action.Id, \"Conflict with server state\", requiresRollback: true);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Data Model Extensions\n\n### 6.1 GameMode Enumeration\n\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Core.Enums\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Defines the synchronization behavior mode for a player's game session.\n    /// </summary>\n    public enum GameMode\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Standard mode: offline-first with periodic background sync.\n        /// Actions succeed locally and sync when connectivity allows.\n        /// </summary>\n        Standard = 0,\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Hardcore mode: real-time server synchronization required.\n        /// Actions require immediate server confirmation to succeed.\n        /// Connectivity is mandatory.\n        /// </summary>\n        Hardcore = 1\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 6.2 Player Entity Extension\n\nIn `TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs`, add:\n\n```csharp\npublic sealed class Player : IObservable<PlayerDomainEvent>\n{\n    // ... existing properties ...\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// The synchronization mode for this player's game session.\n    /// Determines whether actions require real-time server confirmation.\n    /// </summary>\n    public GameMode GameMode { get; private set; } = GameMode.Standard;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Switches the player to Hardcore Mode. Requires active connectivity.\n    /// </summary>\n    public void EnableHardcoreMode(IConnectivityService connectivity)\n    {\n        if (!connectivity.IsOnline)\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                \"Cannot enable Hardcore Mode without active connectivity.\");\n\n        GameMode = GameMode.Hardcore;\n        Publish(new GameModeChangedEvent(Id, GameMode.Hardcore));\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Returns to standard offline-first mode.\n    /// </summary>\n    public void DisableHardcoreMode()\n    {\n        GameMode = GameMode.Standard;\n        Publish(new GameModeChangedEvent(Id, GameMode.Standard));\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Error Handling Considerations\n\n### 7.1 Hardcore Mode Failure Scenarios\n\n| Scenario | Handling Strategy |\n|----------|-------------------|\n| **Network unavailable** | Prevent action; display \"Hardcore Mode requires connectivity\" |\n| **Server timeout** | Rollback local changes; display \"Server did not respond\" |\n| **Server rejection (conflict)** | Rollback local changes; trigger state refresh from server |\n| **Server rejection (validation)** | Rollback local changes; display server error message |\n\n### 7.2 Graceful Degradation\n\nFor players in Hardcore Mode who lose connectivity mid-session:\n1. Detect connectivity loss via `IConnectivityService`\n2. Pause gameplay and display modal: \"Connection lost. Reconnect to continue Hardcore Mode, or switch to Standard Mode.\"\n3. If player chooses Standard Mode, switch `GameMode` and allow continued play\n\n---\n\n## 8. Testing Strategy\n\n### 8.1 Unit Tests\n\n- **OfflineQueuingStrategy**: Verify actions are queued to repository\n- **RealTimeSyncStrategy**: Mock API client; verify success/failure paths\n- **SyncStrategyFactory**: Verify correct strategy selection based on player mode and connectivity\n\n### 8.2 Integration Tests\n\n- End-to-end command execution with both strategies\n- Connectivity transition scenarios\n- Rollback behavior verification for Hardcore Mode failures\n\n---\n\n## 9. Migration Path\n\n### Phase 1: Infrastructure\n1. Create new interfaces and strategy classes\n2. Implement `OfflineQueuingStrategy` wrapping existing behavior\n3. Register services in `ServiceLocator`\n\n### Phase 2: Integration\n1. Modify command handlers to use `ISyncStrategyFactory`\n2. Add `GameMode` property to `Player` entity\n3. Update `ApiClient` with immediate sync endpoint support\n\n### Phase 3: UI/UX\n1. Add Hardcore Mode toggle to player settings\n2. Implement connectivity status indicators\n3. Add error dialogs for Hardcore Mode failures\n\n---\n\n## 10. Conclusion\n\nThis architectural proposal introduces real-time synchronization for Hardcore Mode while preserving the existing offline-first experience through the **Strategy Pattern**. The solution:\n\n- **Adheres to ADR-001** by placing interfaces in Application layer and implementations in Infrastructure layer\n- **Respects ADR-003** by encapsulating existing offline behavior without modification\n- **Follows SOLID principles**, particularly Open/Closed (new modes = new strategies, not if/else)\n- **Centralizes decision logic** in `SyncStrategyFactory`\n- **Minimizes impact** on existing command handlers (add one dependency, few lines of strategy invocation)\n\nThe proposed design is extensible for future sync modes (e.g., \"Tournament Mode\" with enhanced validation) without requiring changes to existing strategies or command handlers.\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- `docs/design/ADR-001-Layered-Architecture.md` - Layered architecture principles\n- `docs/design/ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md` - Current offline-first implementation\n- `docs/architecture.md` - System overview and patterns\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Sync/SyncPlayerActionsCommand.cs` - Current sync implementation\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Networking/ApiClient.cs` - Network abstraction layer\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Common/ServiceLocator.cs` - DI container\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":106,"cache_read_input_tokens":78674,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":106,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"52b95c45-8169-4e21-826d-99ed2f9dc283","uuid":"fc5884d0-8c0a-43b5-ad2c-9b2603eadecd"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NXcvKES5eA5UkiHqjm2PEY","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /logs/agent/solution.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"52b95c45-8169-4e21-826d-99ed2f9dc283","uuid":"731e33c5-aafe-42ba-8610-895872763022","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Architectural Proposal: Real-Time 'Hardcore Mode' Synchronization\n\n**Author:** Lead Architect\n**Date:** February 2026\n**Status:** Proposal\n**Impact:** High - Architectural change affecting sync subsystem\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document proposes an architectural solution to support a new \"Hardcore Mode\" feature that requires real-time server synchronization for competitive players, while preserving the existing offline-first experience for standard players. The solution leverages the **Strategy Pattern** to encapsulate the two synchronization behaviors, maintaining adherence to the Open/Closed Principle and the established layered architecture.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Analysis of Conflict\n\n### 1.1 Current Architecture (ADR-003)\n\nThe TycoonVerse application is fundamentally built on an **offline-first architecture** as documented in `docs/design/ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md`. The key design decisions include:\n\n1. **Client-Side Event Sourcing**: All player actions are captured as domain events and stored locally in SQLite before any server communication occurs (ADR-003, Section 2).\n\n2. **Periodic Background Sync**: The `OfflineSyncManager` (documented in ADR-003:136-261) operates as a singleton that runs a background pump every 15 seconds, batching events and sending them to the server only when connectivity is available.\n\n3. **Queue-First Design**: When actions occur, they are:\n   - First persisted to the local `GameEventLog` SQLite table (ADR-003:99-116)\n   - Marked with a `SyncSessionId` of `NULL` until synced\n   - Eventually pushed via `POST /events/ingest` endpoint\n\n4. **Eventual Consistency**: The system explicitly accepts that client state may diverge from server state temporarily, with CRDT-inspired merge algorithms resolving conflicts later.\n\n### 1.2 Evidence in Source Code\n\nThe offline-first pattern is consistently implemented across the codebase:\n\n**`SyncPlayerActionsCommand.cs:56-69`** - The synchronization is explicitly batch-oriented:\n```csharp\n// Load unsynced actions deterministically ordered by creation date so that\n// the back-end can replay game state in exactly the same way as the client.\nIReadOnlyList<PlayerAction> pending = await _actionRepository\n    .GetPendingAsync(orderByTimestampAsc: true, cancellationToken)\n```\n\n**`ApiClient.cs:97-101`** - Network calls queue locally when offline:\n```csharp\nif (!_connectivity.IsOnline)\n{\n    await QueueOfflineAsync(request, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n    return default;\n}\n```\n\n**`CreateCompanyCommand.cs:107-109`** - Commands persist locally without immediate server sync:\n```csharp\nawait _companyRepository.AddAsync(company, cancellationToken);\nawait _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);\n```\n\n### 1.3 The Fundamental Conflict\n\n**Hardcore Mode Requirement:** All significant player actions must be synchronized with the server in real-time, meaning:\n- The action cannot be considered \"successful\" until the server confirms it\n- Network latency directly impacts gameplay\n- Connectivity is mandatory; actions fail if offline\n\n**Current Architecture Assumption:** Actions succeed locally first, server synchronization happens later, and offline play is fully supported.\n\nThese requirements are mutually exclusive and cannot coexist in the same code path without architectural intervention. Simply adding `if (isHardcoreMode)` checks throughout the codebase would:\n- Violate the **Open/Closed Principle** (classes should be open for extension, closed for modification)\n- Create tight coupling between game mode logic and persistence/sync logic\n- Result in fragile, hard-to-test code with conditional branching in every command handler\n- Make the system resistant to future changes (e.g., adding a third sync mode)\n\n---\n\n## 2. Proposed Architectural Pattern\n\n### 2.1 Primary Pattern: Strategy Pattern\n\nThe **Strategy Pattern** is the optimal solution for this requirement. It allows us to define a family of algorithms (synchronization strategies), encapsulate each one in a separate class, and make them interchangeable at runtime.\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        IActionSyncStrategy                        │\n│  + ExecuteAndSyncAsync(action, ct) : Task<SyncResult>            │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                ▲\n                                │ implements\n                ┌───────────────┴───────────────┐\n                │                               │\n┌───────────────────────────┐   ┌───────────────────────────────────┐\n│  OfflineQueuingStrategy   │   │    RealTimeSyncStrategy           │\n│  - Persists locally first │   │    - Sends to server immediately  │\n│  - Queues for later sync  │   │    - Awaits server confirmation   │\n│  - Returns optimistic OK  │   │    - Fails if offline/rejected    │\n└───────────────────────────┘   └───────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 2.2 Why Strategy Pattern Over Alternatives\n\n| Alternative Approach | Why Rejected |\n|---------------------|--------------|\n| **Naive `if/else` checks** | Violates Open/Closed Principle; scatters game mode logic across entire codebase; makes testing difficult; creates maintenance burden when adding new modes |\n| **Decorator Pattern** | Decorators are better for adding behavior transparently; our strategies have fundamentally different behaviors, not additive enhancements |\n| **Template Method** | Requires inheritance hierarchy; less flexible than composition; harder to switch strategies at runtime |\n| **State Pattern** | Better for state transitions; our need is behavior selection, not state management |\n\n### 2.3 Supporting Pattern: Factory Pattern\n\nA **Sync Strategy Factory** will be responsible for creating and returning the appropriate strategy based on the current player context (game mode). This cleanly separates:\n- **Decision logic** (which strategy to use) - in the factory\n- **Execution logic** (how to sync) - in the strategies\n\n---\n\n## 3. Component Placement and Responsibility\n\n### 3.1 New Components by Layer\n\nFollowing the layered architecture defined in `ADR-001-Layered-Architecture.md`:\n\n#### **Application Layer** (`TycoonVerse.Application`)\n\n| Component | Location | Responsibility |\n|-----------|----------|----------------|\n| `IActionSyncStrategy` | `/Interfaces/Services/IActionSyncStrategy.cs` | Strategy interface defining the sync contract |\n| `ISyncStrategyFactory` | `/Interfaces/Services/ISyncStrategyFactory.cs` | Factory interface for creating strategies |\n| `SyncResult` | `/Common/Models/SyncResult.cs` | Result type returned by sync operations |\n| `PlayerAction` (extended) | `/Features/Sync/PlayerAction.cs` | Already exists; may need minimal extension |\n\n**Interface Definition:**\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Interfaces.Services\n{\n    public interface IActionSyncStrategy\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Executes the given player action and synchronizes it according to\n        /// the strategy's semantics (offline-queued or real-time).\n        /// </summary>\n        Task<SyncResult> ExecuteAndSyncAsync(\n            PlayerAction action,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken);\n    }\n\n    public interface ISyncStrategyFactory\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Returns the appropriate sync strategy based on the player's game mode.\n        /// </summary>\n        IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(Guid playerId);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### **Infrastructure Layer** (`TycoonVerse.Infrastructure`)\n\n| Component | Location | Responsibility |\n|-----------|----------|----------------|\n| `OfflineQueuingStrategy` | `/Services/Sync/OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs` | Implements existing offline-first behavior |\n| `RealTimeSyncStrategy` | `/Services/Sync/RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs` | Implements real-time server sync |\n| `SyncStrategyFactory` | `/Services/Sync/SyncStrategyFactory.cs` | Concrete factory implementation |\n\n#### **Core/Domain Layer** (`TycoonVerse.Core`)\n\n| Component | Location | Responsibility |\n|-----------|----------|----------------|\n| `GameMode` enum | `/Enums/GameMode.cs` | Defines available game modes |\n| `Player.GameMode` property | `/Entities/Player.cs` | Stores player's selected mode |\n\n### 3.2 Existing Classes Requiring Modification\n\nThe following existing classes will need modifications to integrate the new sync strategy:\n\n| File | Modification Required |\n|------|----------------------|\n| `TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs:89-120` | Add `GameMode` property to store player's mode selection |\n| `TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/CreateCompanyCommand.cs:48-121` | Inject `ISyncStrategyFactory`; use strategy after local persistence |\n| `TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/FileIPOCommand.cs:67-162` | Inject `ISyncStrategyFactory`; use strategy after domain operation |\n| `TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Market/GetMarketDataQuery.cs` | Read operations may need real-time data for hardcore mode |\n| `TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Networking/ApiClient.cs:25-278` | Add `SendImmediateAsync()` method for blocking sync calls |\n| `TycoonVerse.Application/Common/ServiceLocator.cs:20-314` | Register new strategy and factory services |\n| `TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Persistence/UnitOfWork.cs` | May need to expose sync hooks for strategy consumption |\n\n### 3.3 Project Structure After Changes\n\n```\nTycoonVerse.Application/\n├── Interfaces/\n│   └── Services/\n│       ├── IActionSyncStrategy.cs      ← NEW\n│       ├── ISyncStrategyFactory.cs     ← NEW\n│       └── IUnitOfWork.cs              (existing)\n├── Common/\n│   ├── Models/\n│   │   └── SyncResult.cs               ← NEW\n│   └── ServiceLocator.cs               (modified)\n└── Features/\n    └── Company/\n        ├── CreateCompanyCommand.cs     (modified)\n        └── FileIPOCommand.cs           (modified)\n\nTycoonVerse.Infrastructure/\n├── Services/\n│   └── Sync/                           ← NEW directory\n│       ├── OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs   ← NEW\n│       ├── RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs     ← NEW\n│       └── SyncStrategyFactory.cs      ← NEW\n└── Networking/\n    └── ApiClient.cs                    (modified)\n\nTycoonVerse.Core/\n├── Entities/\n│   └── Player.cs                       (modified)\n└── Enums/\n    └── GameMode.cs                     ← NEW\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Decision Logic: Where and How\n\n### 4.1 Decision Point Location\n\nThe **`SyncStrategyFactory`** is the single component responsible for determining which synchronization path to use. This follows the **Single Responsibility Principle** - the factory's only job is making this decision.\n\n**Location:** `TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Services/Sync/SyncStrategyFactory.cs`\n\n### 4.2 Decision Flow\n\n```\n┌─────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐\n│  Command        │───>│  SyncStrategyFactory │───>│  IActionSyncStrategy│\n│  Handler        │    │  GetStrategy(playerId)│    │  (concrete impl)    │\n│                 │    └──────────────────────┘    └─────────────────────┘\n└─────────────────┘             │                           │\n                                │                           │\n                                ▼                           ▼\n                    ┌───────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐\n                    │  IPlayerRepository    │    │  ExecuteAndSyncAsync│\n                    │  GetGameMode(playerId)│    │  (action, ct)       │\n                    └───────────────────────┘    └─────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4.3 Decision Algorithm\n\n```csharp\npublic class SyncStrategyFactory : ISyncStrategyFactory\n{\n    private readonly IPlayerRepository _playerRepository;\n    private readonly IConnectivityService _connectivity;\n    private readonly OfflineQueuingStrategy _offlineStrategy;\n    private readonly RealTimeSyncStrategy _realTimeStrategy;\n\n    public IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(Guid playerId)\n    {\n        var player = _playerRepository.GetCached(playerId);\n\n        // Decision logic is centralized HERE\n        if (player?.GameMode == GameMode.Hardcore)\n        {\n            // Real-time mode requires connectivity\n            if (!_connectivity.IsOnline)\n            {\n                throw new HardcoreModeOfflineException(\n                    \"Hardcore Mode requires an active internet connection.\");\n            }\n            return _realTimeStrategy;\n        }\n\n        // Default: standard offline-first behavior\n        return _offlineStrategy;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.4 Integration with Command Handlers\n\nModified command handler pattern (using `CreateCompanyCommand` as example):\n\n```csharp\ninternal sealed class CreateCompanyCommandHandler\n    : IRequestHandler<CreateCompanyCommand, CreateCompanyResult>\n{\n    private readonly ICompanyRepository _companyRepository;\n    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;\n    private readonly ISyncStrategyFactory _syncStrategyFactory;  // NEW\n    private readonly ILogger<CreateCompanyCommandHandler> _logger;\n\n    public async Task<CreateCompanyResult> Handle(\n        CreateCompanyCommand request,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // ... existing validation and domain logic ...\n\n        // Create the domain entity\n        var company = Company.Create(...);\n\n        // Persist locally (unchanged)\n        await _companyRepository.AddAsync(company, cancellationToken);\n        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);\n\n        // NEW: Get sync strategy and execute sync\n        var strategy = _syncStrategyFactory.GetStrategy(request.RequestedByPlayerId);\n        var action = PlayerAction.CreateCompanyAction(company);\n\n        var syncResult = await strategy.ExecuteAndSyncAsync(action, cancellationToken);\n\n        if (!syncResult.IsSuccess && syncResult.RequiresRollback)\n        {\n            // Hardcore mode: server rejected, rollback local changes\n            await _companyRepository.RemoveAsync(company.Id, cancellationToken);\n            await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);\n            throw new ServerRejectionException(syncResult.ErrorMessage);\n        }\n\n        return new CreateCompanyResult(company.Id, company.CreatedAtUtc);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Strategy Implementation Details\n\n### 5.1 OfflineQueuingStrategy (Encapsulates Existing Behavior)\n\nThis strategy encapsulates the current offline-first behavior already documented in ADR-003:\n\n```csharp\npublic class OfflineQueuingStrategy : IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IPlayerActionRepository _actionRepository;\n    private readonly ILogger<OfflineQueuingStrategy> _logger;\n\n    public async Task<SyncResult> ExecuteAndSyncAsync(\n        PlayerAction action,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // Queue for later sync (existing behavior from ADR-003)\n        await _actionRepository.AddPendingAsync(action, cancellationToken);\n\n        _logger.LogDebug(\n            \"Action {ActionId} queued for offline sync.\",\n            action.Id);\n\n        // Always return success - action is persisted locally\n        return SyncResult.QueuedForSync(action.Id);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 5.2 RealTimeSyncStrategy (New Behavior)\n\nThis strategy implements the new real-time synchronization requirement:\n\n```csharp\npublic class RealTimeSyncStrategy : IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IApiClient _apiClient;\n    private readonly ILogger<RealTimeSyncStrategy> _logger;\n    private readonly TimeSpan _timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10);\n\n    public async Task<SyncResult> ExecuteAndSyncAsync(\n        PlayerAction action,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        using var cts = CancellationTokenSource\n            .CreateLinkedTokenSource(cancellationToken);\n        cts.CancelAfter(_timeout);\n\n        try\n        {\n            var request = new ApiRequest(\n                new Uri(\"/events/immediate\", UriKind.Relative),\n                HttpMethod.Post,\n                body: action);\n\n            var response = await _apiClient\n                .SendAsync<ImmediateSyncResponse>(request, cts.Token);\n\n            if (response?.Accepted == true)\n            {\n                _logger.LogInformation(\n                    \"Action {ActionId} confirmed by server in real-time.\",\n                    action.Id);\n                return SyncResult.Confirmed(action.Id, response.ServerSequence);\n            }\n\n            _logger.LogWarning(\n                \"Server rejected action {ActionId}: {Reason}\",\n                action.Id,\n                response?.RejectionReason);\n\n            return SyncResult.Rejected(\n                action.Id,\n                response?.RejectionReason ?? \"Unknown server error\",\n                requiresRollback: true);\n        }\n        catch (OperationCanceledException)\n        {\n            _logger.LogError(\"Real-time sync timed out for action {ActionId}\", action.Id);\n            return SyncResult.Failed(action.Id, \"Server response timeout\", requiresRollback: true);\n        }\n        catch (ApiException ex) when (ex.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.Conflict)\n        {\n            return SyncResult.Rejected(action.Id, \"Conflict with server state\", requiresRollback: true);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Data Model Extensions\n\n### 6.1 GameMode Enumeration\n\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Core.Enums\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Defines the synchronization behavior mode for a player's game session.\n    /// </summary>\n    public enum GameMode\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Standard mode: offline-first with periodic background sync.\n        /// Actions succeed locally and sync when connectivity allows.\n        /// </summary>\n        Standard = 0,\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Hardcore mode: real-time server synchronization required.\n        /// Actions require immediate server confirmation to succeed.\n        /// Connectivity is mandatory.\n        /// </summary>\n        Hardcore = 1\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 6.2 Player Entity Extension\n\nIn `TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs`, add:\n\n```csharp\npublic sealed class Player : IObservable<PlayerDomainEvent>\n{\n    // ... existing properties ...\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// The synchronization mode for this player's game session.\n    /// Determines whether actions require real-time server confirmation.\n    /// </summary>\n    public GameMode GameMode { get; private set; } = GameMode.Standard;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Switches the player to Hardcore Mode. Requires active connectivity.\n    /// </summary>\n    public void EnableHardcoreMode(IConnectivityService connectivity)\n    {\n        if (!connectivity.IsOnline)\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                \"Cannot enable Hardcore Mode without active connectivity.\");\n\n        GameMode = GameMode.Hardcore;\n        Publish(new GameModeChangedEvent(Id, GameMode.Hardcore));\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Returns to standard offline-first mode.\n    /// </summary>\n    public void DisableHardcoreMode()\n    {\n        GameMode = GameMode.Standard;\n        Publish(new GameModeChangedEvent(Id, GameMode.Standard));\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Error Handling Considerations\n\n### 7.1 Hardcore Mode Failure Scenarios\n\n| Scenario | Handling Strategy |\n|----------|-------------------|\n| **Network unavailable** | Prevent action; display \"Hardcore Mode requires connectivity\" |\n| **Server timeout** | Rollback local changes; display \"Server did not respond\" |\n| **Server rejection (conflict)** | Rollback local changes; trigger state refresh from server |\n| **Server rejection (validation)** | Rollback local changes; display server error message |\n\n### 7.2 Graceful Degradation\n\nFor players in Hardcore Mode who lose connectivity mid-session:\n1. Detect connectivity loss via `IConnectivityService`\n2. Pause gameplay and display modal: \"Connection lost. Reconnect to continue Hardcore Mode, or switch to Standard Mode.\"\n3. If player chooses Standard Mode, switch `GameMode` and allow continued play\n\n---\n\n## 8. Testing Strategy\n\n### 8.1 Unit Tests\n\n- **OfflineQueuingStrategy**: Verify actions are queued to repository\n- **RealTimeSyncStrategy**: Mock API client; verify success/failure paths\n- **SyncStrategyFactory**: Verify correct strategy selection based on player mode and connectivity\n\n### 8.2 Integration Tests\n\n- End-to-end command execution with both strategies\n- Connectivity transition scenarios\n- Rollback behavior verification for Hardcore Mode failures\n\n---\n\n## 9. Conclusion\n\nThis architectural proposal introduces real-time synchronization for Hardcore Mode while preserving the existing offline-first experience through the **Strategy Pattern**. The solution:\n\n- **Adheres to ADR-001** by placing interfaces in Application layer and implementations in Infrastructure layer\n- **Respects ADR-003** by encapsulating existing offline behavior without modification\n- **Follows SOLID principles**, particularly Open/Closed (new modes = new strategies, not if/else)\n- **Centralizes decision logic** in `SyncStrategyFactory`\n- **Minimizes impact** on existing command handlers (add one dependency, few lines of strategy invocation)\n\nThe proposed design is extensible for future sync modes (e.g., \"Tournament Mode\" with enhanced validation) without requiring changes to existing strategies or command handlers.\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- `docs/design/ADR-001-Layered-Architecture.md` - Layered architecture principles\n- `docs/design/ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md` - Current offline-first implementation\n- `docs/architecture.md` - System overview and patterns\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Sync/SyncPlayerActionsCommand.cs` - Current sync implementation\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Networking/ApiClient.cs` - Network abstraction layer\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Common/ServiceLocator.cs` - DI container\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
